## Part 3: Docs Audit and References (checkrm)

### 3.1 Authority and Scope
- `INSTRUCTIONS.md` Part 1 and Part 2 here are authoritative. Unreadable: stop, report. NEVER work from memory.
- Docs and catalog maintenance ONLY. NEVER change gameplay code, manifests, UUIDs, versions, deps, pack settings, assets, workflows, release config unless explicitly asked. "Documentation-only" = touch none of those.
- Modes: repo-wide (`/checkrm`, "all projects", no single project named) | single project (name/slug/folder, or create/repair/update one project's docs).
- ALWAYS discover real folder structure first. NEVER assume a path or substitute a similar project. Report ambiguity/missing target.

### 3.2 Procedure
1. Discover: target project, README, DESCRIPTION, manifests, scripts, config, entities, items, blocks, recipes, functions, loot tables, lang files, assets; parent catalog, builds README.
2. Audit by reading (agents do not run `scripts/check_readme_status.py`; the `.sh` runs it). It checks: missing/incomplete docs, add-on READMEs with product info, short map/script READMEs, broken relative links, orphan build dirs. Cover the same ground by hand and report.
3. Verify facts BEFORE writing: what it does, features, activation/config, persistence, packs/deps, versions, limits, achievements/experiments (1.2). NEVER infer a feature from a name. Separate implemented from planned. Verify vanilla names/sprites against 3.3-3.4.
4. References: before creating/rewriting README or DESCRIPTION read category catalog, builds README, 2-3 well-documented same-category projects. Reuse conventions, NEVER project-specific claims. Verify their links/images/claims first.
5. Write per `DOCS_RULES.md`. NEVER add an unimplemented feature because requested. Omit install steps if asked.
6. Images/badges/links: README none. DESCRIPTION 2 template badges + profile banners, no other badges. Other images: item sprites, recipe diagrams, snapshots from the pack's `assets/` folders (see 1.5). Confirm assets exist, absolute raw URLs, verify link targets. No placeholders.
7. Sync catalogs and builds: check category catalog, builds README. Update only relevant entries. Search first (no duplicates). Keep ordering. Inspect build scripts before running. The `.sh` or CI runs `scripts/auto_bump_and_build.py --project "<Project-Name>"` (never changes manifest versions). NEVER claim build/sync done if failed or not run.
8. Verify: re-read whole edited file (headings, lists, links); each feature has source evidence, no verified content lost; the `.sh` runs `scripts/validate_manifest_titles.py --project <path>` and `scripts/check_readme_status.py` (expect 0 needing update, `[LINK INTEGRITY] All relative links valid`); LOCK marker for `MCPEDL/Addons/`; review diff for scope. State any check not run.
9. Report under **Changelog** (`INSTRUCTIONS.md` Reply Format): projects audited/updated, files changed, commands + results, missing assets/links/unverifiable info, remaining work. NEVER report success for an unrun or failed check.
- Bulk remake (run by the `.sh` or `remake-docs.yml`, never by the agent): `python3 scripts/check_readme_status.py --remake standardize --all --check` previews; without `--check` writes. `standardize` keeps text, refreshes READMEs as version-free pages, strips versions from DESCRIPTION titles, fixes structure, adds missing CHANGELOG entry. `--remake rebuild --project <Name>` regenerates generic DESCRIPTION/README from source (`--all` needs `--confirm-rebuild`). Defaults (Verification, Achievements, Experimental features) are placeholders: replace with verified facts. `remake-docs.yml` = dry run + PR.

### 3.3 Minecraft Wiki Item Reference
- https://minecraft.wiki/w/Item = official names, grouping, sprite look. ALWAYS together with Samples, NEVER instead.
- Ids, textures, recipes, components: Samples. Display names: Wiki Bedrock name, confirmed against Samples `resource_pack/texts/en_US.lang` (`item.<id>.name`, `tile.<id>.name`). Lang file = in-game truth if Wiki unreachable/disagrees; report disagreement.
- NEVER title-case an identifier (`experience_bottle` = Bottle o' Enchanting, `hay_block` = Hay Bale, `ender_eye` = Eye of Ender, `wooden_door` = Oak Door, `golden_rail` = Powered Rail, `magma` = Magma Block).
- Edition tags `[BE only]`/`[JE only]`. NEVER document a Java-only item as available (Spectral Arrow is not Bedrock).

### 3.4 Mojang Bedrock Samples Reference
- Wiki sprite names: `Invicon_<Title_Case_Name>.png`, `ItemSprite_<lowercase-hyphenated-name>.png`. Look up by Wiki name, NEVER identifier. Fetch with available web tool; shell blocked: report, NEVER route around, fall back to Samples. Image license rules: 1.5.
- Wiki, `en_US.lang`, Samples lag each other. ALWAYS check Samples `version.json`, state the version a claim was verified against.
- Samples = truth for vanilla ids, textures, recipes, components, entities, formats (1.1 source priority). Root `BEDROCK_SAMPLES_ROOT` (workflow clones to `/tmp/bedrock-samples`).
- Layout: `resource_pack/` (`textures/items/`, `textures/blocks/`, `textures/models/armor/`, `textures/ui/`, `attachables/`, `texts/`), `behavior_pack/` (`items/`, `entities/`, `recipes/`, `loot_tables/`).
- Content changes per release: ALWAYS verify checked-out version, confirm each path exists. NEVER hardcode values (armor defense, durability) from memory.
- Ids != texture filenames. Inventory all recipes, map every ingredient, report unresolved together. NEVER placeholder.
- `textures/item_texture.json`, `terrain_texture.json` start with a `//` comment line: strip before parsing. Many vanilla items have no id-keyed entry; many block textures are `.tga`: check both extensions before calling missing.
- Block icons render in game from top+side textures: build isometric view from real faces for cube sprites.
- An id valid elsewhere may not exist in Bedrock (`oak_door` = `wooden_door`). Confirm id appears in Samples recipes/loot tables/trades before documenting.
- Custom items use the add-on's own RP textures. Vanilla `behavior_pack/recipes/` = reference for recipe format, group tags, ingredient ids.
- Checkout missing/asset absent: report. Only GitHub reachable: `git clone --filter=blob:none --sparse` then `git sparse-checkout set --no-cone <paths>`.
- Scale 16x16 textures with nearest-neighbor (`Image.Resampling.NEAREST`).
````

## FILE: scripts/build_payload.py (925 lines, 47866 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Project payload builder v9 for ALNStudios/MCPE-Curseforges.

One script for all four project types: addon, map, script, minigame.
It installs the mandatory repo assets that are missing, runs every structural check,
builds the ZIP, writes payload.b64 (one line) and round-trips it.
scripts/import_project_payload.py imports this same file, so the agent-side checks and
the repo-side checks cannot drift apart.

Usage:  python build_payload.py build/Project-Name [--out payload.b64] [--check-only]
Needs build/Project-Name/project.json:  {"type": "addon|map|script|minigame", "name": "Display Name"}
Structural checks only: never proof that anything works in Minecraft.
Exit codes: 0 ok, 1 checks failed, 2 packaging or round-trip failed.
"""
import argparse, base64, hashlib, io, json, re, shutil, struct, subprocess, sys, tempfile, zipfile
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

TYPES = ("addon", "map", "script", "minigame")
MODES = ("Creative", "Survival", "Hardcore")
DEST_DIR = {"addon": "Addons", "map": "Maps", "script": "Script", "minigame": "Minigame"}
MIN_ITEM_FORMAT = [1, 20, 80]
CONTROLLER_LABELS = ("Start", "How to play")
CONTROLLER_JS_REL = "behavior_pack/scripts/controller.js"
PIXELIFY_JS_REL = "behavior_pack/scripts/lib/pixelify.js"
PIXELIFY_JS = r'''// Pixelify Sans glyph mapper (page F0). Spaces and section-sign color codes stay vanilla.
const PAGE = 0x00F0;
export function pixelify(text) {
  let out = "";
  for (const ch of String(text)) {
    const c = ch.codePointAt(0);
    out += c > 0x20 && c < 0x7F ? String.fromCharCode(PAGE + c) : ch;
  }
  return out;
}
'''
SKIN_REL = "assets/aln-ui-redesign"  # repo copy, used by the importer (binary files are never in a payload)
SKIN_FILES = (("font/glyph_F0.png", "resource_pack/font/glyph_F0.png"),
              ("font/PixelifySans-OFL.txt", "resource_pack/font/PixelifySans-OFL.txt"))
SKIN_ABOUT = ("about_icon.png", "about_icon.jpg")  # shipped as resource_pack/textures/ui/aln/<same name>
CONTROLLER_JS = r'''import { world, system, ItemStack, EntityComponentTypes, GameMode } from "@minecraft/server";
import { ActionFormData, FormCancelationReason } from "@minecraft/server-ui";
import { pixelify } from "./lib/pixelify.js";

const CONTROLLER_ID = "allenscreations:controller";
const STATE_KEY = "game_state";
const OPERATOR_LEVEL = 2;
const ABOUT_ICON = "textures/ui/aln/about_icon";
const EXIT_ICON = "textures/items/door_wood";

let config = null;
let registered = false;

const getState = () => {
  const v = world.getDynamicProperty(STATE_KEY);
  return typeof v === "string" ? v : "idle";
};
const setState = (s) => world.setDynamicProperty(STATE_KEY, s);
const isCreative = (player) => player.getGameMode() === GameMode.Creative;
const isHost = (player) => (typeof player.isOp === "function" ? player.isOp() : player.playerPermissionLevel === OPERATOR_LEVEL);
const containerOf = (player) => player.getComponent(EntityComponentTypes.Inventory)?.container;

const plain = (s) => String(s ?? "").replace(/§./g, "");
const bold = (s) => `§l${pixelify(plain(s))}§r`;
const mainTitle = () => `§l§6${pixelify("@ALNStudios")} §e${pixelify(plain(config.title))}§r ${pixelify(`(v${config.version})`)}`;
const tell = (player, color, text) => {
  try {
    player.onScreenDisplay.setActionBar(`${color}${text}`);
  } catch (e) {}
};

async function show(form, player) {
  try {
    return await form.show(player);
  } catch (e) {
    return undefined;
  }
}

function hasController(player) {
  const inv = containerOf(player);
  if (!inv) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < inv.size; i++) {
    if (inv.getItem(i)?.typeId === CONTROLLER_ID) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function giveController(player) {
  if (hasController(player)) return;
  containerOf(player)?.addItem(new ItemStack(CONTROLLER_ID, 1));
}

function consumeController(player) {
  if (isCreative(player)) return;
  const inv = containerOf(player);
  if (!inv) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < inv.size; i++) {
    if (inv.getItem(i)?.typeId === CONTROLLER_ID) {
      inv.setItem(i, undefined);
      player.playSound("random.break");
      tell(player, "§e", "The Controller broke.");
      return;
    }
  }
}

function runStart(player) {
  if (getState() === "running") {
    tell(player, "§e", "Already running.");
    return;
  }
  setState("running");
  if (config.consumeOnStart) consumeController(player);
  try {
    config.onStart(player);
  } catch (e) {
    setState("idle");
    giveController(player);
    tell(player, "§c", "The game could not start.");
  }
}

export function endGame() {
  if (getState() !== "running") return;
  setState("ended");
  try {
    config?.onEnd();
  } catch (e) {
    world.sendMessage("The game ended with an error.");
  }
  for (const p of world.getAllPlayers()) if (isHost(p)) giveController(p);
}

async function openStart(player) {
  if (getState() === "running") {
    tell(player, "§e", "Already running.");
    return;
  }
  const form = new ActionFormData()
    .title(bold("How to play"))
    .body(bold(config.howToPlay))
    .button(bold("Start Game"))
    .button(bold("Back"));
  const res = await show(form, player);
  if (!res || res.canceled) return;
  if (res.selection === 0) runStart(player);
  else openController(player);
}

async function openAbout(player) {
  const body = [
    "Creator: ALNStudios",
    `${plain(config.title)} v${config.version}`,
    plain(config.about),
    "CurseForge: allenscreations",
    "MCPEDL: alnstudios",
    "Not affiliated with Mojang.",
  ].join("\n");
  const form = new ActionFormData().title(bold("About ALNStudios")).body(bold(body)).button(bold("Back"));
  const res = await show(form, player);
  if (res && !res.canceled) openController(player);
}

async function confirmEnd(player) {
  if (getState() !== "running") {
    tell(player, "§e", "No game is running.");
    return;
  }
  const form = new ActionFormData()
    .title(bold("End game?"))
    .body(bold("This stops the game that is running now."))
    .button(bold("End Game"))
    .button(bold("Back"));
  const res = await show(form, player);
  if (!res || res.canceled) return;
  if (res.selection === 0) {
    if (getState() !== "running") {
      tell(player, "§e", "The game already ended.");
      return;
    }
    world.sendMessage(`${player.name} ended the game.`);
    endGame();
  } else {
    openController(player);
  }
}

async function openController(player, attempt = 0) {
  const running = getState() === "running";
  const note = running
    ? "End Game stops the current game."
    : config.consumeOnStart
      ? (isCreative(player) ? "Creative: the Controller is kept." : "Start uses up the Controller.")
      : "The Controller is kept.";
  const form = new ActionFormData()
    .title(mainTitle())
    .body(bold(`${config.gameInfo}\n\nState: ${getState()}\n${note}`))
    .button(bold(running ? "End Game" : "Start"))
    .button(bold("About"), ABOUT_ICON)
    .button(bold("Exit"), EXIT_ICON);
  const res = await show(form, player);
  if (!res) return;
  if (res.canceled) {
    if (res.cancelationReason === FormCancelationReason.UserBusy && attempt < 5) {
      system.runTimeout(() => openController(player, attempt + 1), 10);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (res.selection === 0) {
    if (running) confirmEnd(player);
    else openStart(player);
  } else if (res.selection === 1) {
    openAbout(player);
  }
}

export function registerController(options) {
  if (registered) return;
  registered = true;
  config = {
    title: options?.title ?? "Controller",
    version: options?.version ?? "1.0.0",
    about: options?.about ?? "",
    gameInfo: options?.gameInfo ?? "",
    howToPlay: options?.howToPlay ?? "",
    consumeOnStart: options?.consumeOnStart === true,
    onStart: options?.onStart ?? (() => {}),
    onEnd: options?.onEnd ?? (() => {}),
  };

  world.afterEvents.itemUse.subscribe(({ source, itemStack }) => {
    if (itemStack?.typeId === CONTROLLER_ID && isHost(source)) openController(source);
  });

  system.afterEvents.scriptEventReceive.subscribe(({ id }) => {
    if (id === "allenscreations:end") endGame();
  });

  world.afterEvents.worldLoad.subscribe(() => {
    if (getState() === "running") setState("idle");
    for (const p of world.getAllPlayers()) if (isHost(p)) giveController(p);
  });

  world.afterEvents.playerSpawn.subscribe(({ player, initialSpawn }) => {
    if (!isHost(player)) return;
    if (config.consumeOnStart) {
      if (initialSpawn && (isCreative(player) || getState() !== "running")) giveController(player);
      return;
    }
    giveController(player);
  });
}

ASSET_DIR = "Mcpeworlds/assets/resources_pack/textures/items"  # repo copies, used by the importer

                "behavior_pack/functions/void_cleaner.mcfunction", "behavior_pack/functions/oneblock_setup.mcfunction",

def _png(b64, sha):
    data = base64.b64decode(b64, validate=True)
    assert hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() == sha, "embedded PNG is corrupt"
    return data


def pack_slug(name):
    slug = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", str(name).lower()).strip("_")
    return slug or "oneblock"


def read_project(root):
    try:
        return json.loads((Path(root) / "project.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    except Exception:
        return None


def wants_controller(pj):
    return isinstance(pj, dict) and pj.get("type") == "minigame"


def item_format_version(root):
    try:
        m = json.loads((Path(root) / "behavior_pack/manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        mev = m["header"]["min_engine_version"]
        if isinstance(mev, list) and len(mev) == 3 and all(isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) for x in mev) and mev != [0, 0, 0]:
            return ".".join(str(x) for x in max(mev, MIN_ITEM_FORMAT))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _item(template, root):
    fv = item_format_version(root)
    if fv is None:
        sys.exit("Set behavior_pack/manifest.json header.min_engine_version to the looked-up game version before running this script: the shipped item JSON takes its format_version from it.")
    return {**template, "format_version": fv}


def _write_json(p, data):
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def _install_icon(root, key, png_bytes, entry, label, base_texture):
    png = root / f"resource_pack/textures/items/{key}.png"
    if not png.exists():
        png.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        png.write_bytes(png_bytes)
        print(f"installed  {png.relative_to(root).as_posix()}")
    t = root / "resource_pack/textures/item_texture.json"
    if not t.exists():
        _write_json(t, base_texture)
        print("installed  resource_pack/textures/item_texture.json")
        return
    try:
        data = json.loads(t.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    except Exception:
        return  # run_checks reports invalid JSON
    if data.get("texture_name") != "atlas.items" or data.get("texture_data", {}).get(key) != entry:
        data["texture_name"] = "atlas.items"
        data.setdefault("texture_data", {})[key] = entry
        _write_json(t, data)
        print(f"repaired   item_texture.json {key} entry")


def install_skin(root, repo_root):
    """Controller projects: copy the ALN UI Redesign font and About icon from the repo into the Resource Pack
    (copy, never link). Existing files stay. Returns the list of problems (empty = fine)."""
    pj = read_project(root)
    if not isinstance(pj, dict) or not wants_controller(pj):
        return []
    src = Path(repo_root) / SKIN_REL
    pairs = list(SKIN_FILES)
    about = next((n for n in SKIN_ABOUT if (src / n).is_file()), None)
    if about:
        pairs.append((about, f"resource_pack/textures/ui/aln/{about}"))
    problems = []
    for s, d in pairs:
        if not (src / s).is_file():
            problems.append(f"repo skin file {SKIN_REL}/{s} is missing")
            continue
        out = root / d
        if not out.exists():
            out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            out.write_bytes((src / s).read_bytes())
            print("installed  " + d)
    if not about:
        problems.append(f"repo skin file {SKIN_REL}/about_icon.png or about_icon.jpg is missing")
    return problems


def install_assets(root):
    """Create the mandatory icon + item files if missing. Never overwrites an existing PNG."""
    pj = read_project(root)
    if not isinstance(pj, dict):
        return
    if pj.get("type") == "map":
        base = {"resource_pack_name": pack_slug(pj.get("name")), "texture_name": "atlas.items",
                "texture_data": {"trader_ledger": LEDGER_ENTRY}}
        _install_icon(root, "trader_ledger", _png(LEDGER_PNG_B64, LEDGER_SHA256), LEDGER_ENTRY, "ledger", base)
        i = root / "behavior_pack/items/trader_ledger.json"
        if not i.exists():
            _write_json(i, _item(LEDGER_ITEM, root))
            print("installed  behavior_pack/items/trader_ledger.json")
    elif wants_controller(pj):
        base = {"resource_pack_name": "allenscreations", "texture_name": "atlas.items",
                "texture_data": {"controller": CONTROLLER_ENTRY}}
        _install_icon(root, "controller", _png(CONTROLLER_PNG_B64, CONTROLLER_SHA256), CONTROLLER_ENTRY, "controller", base)
        i = root / "behavior_pack/items/controller.json"
        if not i.exists():
            _write_json(i, _item(CONTROLLER_ITEM, root))
            print("installed  behavior_pack/items/controller.json")
        g = root / "behavior_pack/functions/give_controller.mcfunction"
        if not g.exists():
            g.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            g.write_text(GIVE_LINE + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
            print("installed  behavior_pack/functions/give_controller.mcfunction")
        e = root / "behavior_pack/functions/end_game.mcfunction"
        if not e.exists():
            e.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            e.write_text(END_LINE + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
            print("installed  behavior_pack/functions/end_game.mcfunction")
        mjs = root / "behavior_pack/scripts/main.js"
        cjs = root / CONTROLLER_JS_REL
        pjs = root / PIXELIFY_JS_REL
        if not pjs.exists() and mjs.is_file() and "registerController(" in mjs.read_text(encoding="utf-8"):
            pjs.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            pjs.write_text(PIXELIFY_JS, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
            print("installed  " + PIXELIFY_JS_REL)
        if not cjs.exists() and mjs.is_file() and "registerController(" in mjs.read_text(encoding="utf-8"):
            cjs.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            cjs.write_text(CONTROLLER_JS, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
            print("installed  " + CONTROLLER_JS_REL)


def _png_size(d):
    if d[:8] != b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" or len(d) < 24:
        return None
    return struct.unpack(">II", d[16:24])


def _has_comment(src):
    i, n, q = 0, len(src), None
    while i < n:
        c = src[i]
        if q:
            if c == "\\":
                i += 2
                continue
            if c == q or (q != "`" and c == "\n"):
                q = None
            i += 1
            continue
        if c in "\"'`":
            q = c
        elif c == "/" and i + 1 < n and src[i + 1] in "/*":
            return True
        i += 1
    return False


def _node_syntax(path):
    """Return (ran, error). Uses node --check on a .mjs copy; ran is False when node is missing."""
    node = shutil.which("node")
    if not node:
        return False, ""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
        tmp = Path(t) / "check.mjs"
        shutil.copyfile(path, tmp)
        r = subprocess.run([node, "--check", str(tmp)], capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode == 0:
        return True, ""
    if "SyntaxError" not in r.stderr:
        return False, ""
    errs = [ln for ln in r.stderr.splitlines() if "Error" in ln]
    return True, (errs[-1] if errs else "syntax error").strip()


def run_checks(root, repo_root=None, folder=None):
    """Return (fails, warns, file_count, total_bytes).
    repo_root (importer only): compare icons to the repo copies.
    folder (importer only): the folder name the project will get."""
    root = Path(root)
    fails, warns = [], []

    def check(ok, msg):
        if not ok:
            fails.append(msg)

    def jload(rel, strict=True):
        try:
            return json.loads((root / rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        except Exception as exc:
            (fails if strict else warns).append(f"{rel}: missing or invalid JSON ({exc})")
            return None

    pj = jload("project.json")
    ptype = pj.get("type") if isinstance(pj, dict) else None
    check(ptype in TYPES, f"project.json: type must be one of {', '.join(TYPES)}")
    if isinstance(pj, dict):
        check(isinstance(pj.get("name"), str) and bool(pj["name"].strip()), "project.json needs a non-empty name")
        check(pj.get("controller", True) in (True, False), "project.json: controller must be true or false")
        if ptype == "minigame":
            check(pj.get("controller", True) is not False, "minigame projects must use the Controller item")
    strict = {"project.json", "behavior_pack/manifest.json", "resource_pack/manifest.json", "behavior_pack/functions/tick.json",
              "resource_pack/textures/item_texture.json", "behavior_pack/items/trader_ledger.json",
              "behavior_pack/items/controller.json", "oneblock.json"}

    files = [p for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()]
    total = 0
    check(len(files) <= MAX_FILES, f"{len(files)} files (max {MAX_FILES})")
    for p in files:
        rel = p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        low = PurePosixPath(rel.lower())
        size = p.stat().st_size
        total += size
        check(size <= MAX_FILE, f"{rel}: over 5 MB")
        check(low.suffix in ALLOWED, f"{rel}: file type not allowed")
        check("db" not in low.parts and low.name != "level.dat", f"{rel}: world data not allowed")
        check(low.name not in ("world_behavior_packs.json", "world_resource_packs.json"), f"{rel}: link files not allowed")
        check(not rel.lower().endswith(BLOCKED), f"{rel}: nested archive")
        check(not p.is_symlink(), f"{rel}: symlink")
        check(not rel.startswith("Mcpeworlds/"), f"{rel}: Mcpeworlds paths must not be shipped")
        check(low.name != "test_world.json", f"{rel}: test worlds were removed; delete test_world.json")
        if low.suffix == ".json" and rel not in strict and not rel.startswith("behavior_pack/recipes/"):
            jload(rel, strict=False)
    check(total <= MAX_TOTAL, f"total {total} bytes (max 25 MB)")
    for rel in COMMON_REQUIRED:
        check((root / rel).is_file(), f"missing required file: {rel}")
    controller = wants_controller(pj)
    if ptype == "map":
        for rel in MAP_REQUIRED:
            check((root / rel).is_file(), f"missing required file: {rel}")
    if controller:
        for rel in CONTROLLER_REQUIRED:
            check((root / rel).is_file(), f"missing required file: {rel}")
    if ptype in ("script", "minigame"):
        check((root / "behavior_pack/scripts/main.js").is_file(), "missing required file: behavior_pack/scripts/main.js")
    if ptype == "script":
        check(not (root / "resource_pack").exists(), "script projects must be Behavior Pack-only so the package is a .mcpack")
    if ptype == "minigame":
        check((root / "resource_pack/manifest.json").is_file(), "minigame projects need a Resource Pack for the Controller item")

    # oneblock.json is optional in the payload (the importer writes it for maps); if present it must agree
    if (root / "oneblock.json").is_file() and ptype == "map":
        cfg = jload("oneblock.json")
        if isinstance(cfg, dict):
            check(cfg.get("enabled") is True, "oneblock.json enabled must be true")
            if isinstance(pj, dict):
                check(cfg.get("name") == pj.get("name"), "oneblock.json name must equal project.json name")

    # tick.json: required for maps, validated whenever present
    tick_p = root / "behavior_pack/functions/tick.json"
    if ptype == "map" or tick_p.is_file():
        tick = jload("behavior_pack/functions/tick.json")
        vals = (tick or {}).get("values")
        check(isinstance(vals, list) and bool(vals), "tick.json needs a non-empty values list")
        if isinstance(vals, list) and vals:
            if ptype == "map":
                check(vals[0] == "void_cleaner", "tick.json: first entry must be void_cleaner")
            for v in vals:
                check((root / f"behavior_pack/functions/{v}.mcfunction").is_file(), f"tick.json names {v} but its .mcfunction is missing")
            if "oneblock_setup" in vals:
                warns.append("oneblock_setup is listed in tick.json and will run every tick; intended?")
    if ptype == "map":
        cl = root / "behavior_pack/functions/void_cleaner.mcfunction"
        if cl.is_file():
            lines = cl.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
            check(CLEANER in lines, "void_cleaner.mcfunction lacks the exact required fill line")
            cmds = [x for x in lines if x.strip() and not x.lstrip().startswith("#")]
            check(bool(cmds) and cmds[0] == CLEANER, "the required fill line must be the first command")
            check(bool(cmds) and re.search(r"\btag\b.*\badd\s+void_cleaned\b", cmds[-1]) is not None,
                  "void_cleaner.mcfunction: the last command must add the void_cleaned tag, otherwise every command repeats every tick")
            check(bool(cmds) and CORE_GUARD.search(cmds[-1]) is not None,
                  "void_cleaner.mcfunction: the last command must add the void_cleaned tag only if the core Bedrock exists "
                  "(execute as @a[tag=!void_cleaned] if block <x> <y> <z> bedrock run tag @s add void_cleaned), "
                  "otherwise a fill on unloaded chunks is never retried")

    uuids, versions, huuid, bpm, mevs = [], {}, {}, {}, {}
    labels = ["behavior_pack"] + (["resource_pack"] if (root / "resource_pack/manifest.json").is_file() else [])
    for label in labels:
        m = jload(f"{label}/manifest.json")
        if not isinstance(m, dict):
            continue
        if label == "behavior_pack":
            bpm = m
        check(m.get("format_version") == 2, f"{label}: format_version must be 2")
        h = m.get("header") or {}
        uuid, ver, name, desc = h.get("uuid"), h.get("version"), h.get("name", ""), h.get("description", "")
        check(isinstance(uuid, str) and bool(UUID_V4.match(uuid)), f"{label}: header.uuid missing or not a v4 UUID")
        check(isinstance(ver, list) and len(ver) == 3 and all(isinstance(x, int) for x in ver), f"{label}: header.version must be [x,y,z]")
        mev = h.get("min_engine_version")
        if not (isinstance(mev, list) and len(mev) == 3 and all(isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) for x in mev)) or mev == [0, 0, 0]:
            fails.append(f"{label}: header.min_engine_version must be 3 integers (the looked-up game version), not a placeholder")
        mevs[label] = mev
        t = TITLE.match(name) if isinstance(name, str) else None
        check(bool(t), f"{label}: header.name must end with one (vX.Y.Z)")
        if t and isinstance(ver, list):
            check([int(x) for x in t.groups()] == ver, f"{label}: title version does not match header.version")
            check(name.count("(v") == 1, f"{label}: duplicated version suffix in name")
        check(isinstance(desc, str) and desc.count(ATTRIBUTION) == 1, f"{label}: description needs '{ATTRIBUTION}' exactly once")
        check(ATTRIBUTION not in str(name), f"{label}: keep attribution out of the name")
        uuids.append(uuid)
        mods = m.get("modules") or []
        check(bool(mods), f"{label}: no modules")
        for mod in mods:
            uuids.append(mod.get("uuid"))
            check(isinstance(mod.get("uuid"), str) and bool(UUID_V4.match(mod["uuid"])), f"{label}: module uuid missing or invalid")
            if mod.get("type") == "script":
                entry = mod.get("entry")
                check(isinstance(entry, str) and (root / label / entry).is_file(), f"{label}: script module entry file missing")
                check(mod.get("language") == "javascript", f"{label}: script module language must be javascript")
        versions[label], huuid[label] = ver, uuid
        if label == "resource_pack":
            check(any(x.get("type") == "resources" for x in mods), "resource_pack needs a 'resources' module")
    check(len(uuids) == len(set(uuids)), "UUIDs must all be unique (headers and modules)")
    if repo_root:
        mine = {u.lower() for u in uuids if isinstance(u, str)}
        for mp in sorted((Path(repo_root) / "MCPEDL").glob("*/*/*/manifest.json")):
            proj = mp.parent.parent
            if folder and proj.name == folder and proj.parent.name == DEST_DIR.get(ptype):
                continue
            try:
                other = json.loads(mp.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
            except Exception:
                continue
            ids = [(other.get("header") or {}).get("uuid")] + [m.get("uuid") for m in other.get("modules") or []]
            for u in ids:
                if isinstance(u, str) and u.lower() in mine:
                    fails.append(f"UUID {u} is already used by MCPEDL/{proj.parent.name}/{proj.name}: generate fresh UUIDs")
    if "resource_pack" in versions:
        check(versions.get("behavior_pack") == versions.get("resource_pack"), "behavior and resource pack versions must match")
        dep = [d for d in bpm.get("dependencies") or [] if d.get("uuid") == huuid.get("resource_pack")]
        check(bool(dep), "behavior pack must depend on the resource pack header.uuid")
        if dep:
            check(dep[0].get("version") == versions.get("resource_pack"), "resource pack dependency version must match")
    modnames = {}
    for d in bpm.get("dependencies") or []:
        if "module_name" in d:
            modnames[d["module_name"]] = d.get("version")
            check(isinstance(d.get("version"), str) and bool(d.get("version")), f"dependency {d['module_name']} needs a version string")
            check(d["module_name"] in ALLOWED_MODULES, f"dependency {d['module_name']} is not allowed (allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_MODULES)})")
        if any(w in str(d.get("version", "")).lower() for w in ("beta", "rc", "preview")):
            warns.append(f"preview/beta dependency {d.get('module_name')}: allowed only if a requested feature needs it; say so in the README")
    has_script = any(x.get("type") == "script" for x in bpm.get("modules") or [])
    if ptype in ("script", "minigame"):
        check(has_script, f"{ptype} project: behavior_pack manifest needs a script module")
    if has_script:
        check("@minecraft/server" in modnames, "script module needs a @minecraft/server dependency")
    if controller:
        check("@minecraft/server-ui" in modnames, "controller needs a @minecraft/server-ui dependency")
    mev_bp = mevs.get("behavior_pack")
    if isinstance(mev_bp, list) and len(mev_bp) == 3 and all(isinstance(x, int) for x in mev_bp) and mev_bp != [0, 0, 0]:
        for item_rel in ("behavior_pack/items/trader_ledger.json", "behavior_pack/items/controller.json"):
            ip = root / item_rel
            if not ip.is_file():
                continue
            try:
                fv = [int(x) for x in str(json.loads(ip.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")).get("format_version", "")).split(".")]
            except Exception:
                continue
            if len(fv) == 3 and fv > mev_bp:
                warns.append(f"{item_rel}: format_version {'.'.join(map(str, fv))} is newer than min_engine_version {'.'.join(map(str, mev_bp))}: raise min_engine_version")

    rec = root / "behavior_pack/recipes"
    for p in rec.glob("**/*.json") if rec.is_dir() else []:
        rrel = p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        try:
            rdata = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        except Exception as exc:
            fails.append(f"{rrel}: invalid JSON ({exc})")
            continue
        rkey = next((k for k in CRAFTING_RECIPES if isinstance(rdata, dict) and k in rdata), None)
        if rkey is None:
            continue
        body = rdata[rkey] if isinstance(rdata[rkey], dict) else {}
        unlock = body.get("unlock")
        entries = unlock if isinstance(unlock, list) else [unlock]
        check(any(isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("context") == "AlwaysUnlocked" for e in entries),
              f"{rrel}: missing the unlock context AlwaysUnlocked inside {rkey}")

    def icon_checks(key, ident, item_rel, want_sha, size):
        png = root / f"resource_pack/textures/items/{key}.png"
        if png.is_file():
            d = png.read_bytes()
            ref = (Path(repo_root) / ASSET_DIR / f"{key}.png") if repo_root else None
            if ref is not None:
                check(ref.is_file(), f"repo asset {ASSET_DIR}/{key}.png is missing")
                if ref.is_file():
                    check(d == ref.read_bytes(), f"{key}.png is not a byte-for-byte copy of the repo asset")
            else:
                check(hashlib.sha256(d).hexdigest() == want_sha, f"{key}.png is not a byte-for-byte copy (SHA-256 mismatch)")
            check(_png_size(d) == size, f"{key}.png must be a {size[0]}x{size[1]} PNG")
        its = jload("resource_pack/textures/item_texture.json") or {}
        check(its.get("texture_name") == "atlas.items", "item_texture.json: texture_name must be atlas.items")
        check(((its.get("texture_data") or {}).get(key) or {}).get("textures") == f"textures/items/{key}",
              f"item_texture.json: {key} entry missing or wrong path")
        item = (jload(item_rel) or {}).get("minecraft:item") or {}
        check((item.get("description") or {}).get("identifier") == ident, f"{item_rel}: identifier must be {ident}")
        icon = ((item.get("components") or {}).get("minecraft:icon") or {}).get("textures", {}).get("default")
        check(icon == key, f"{item_rel}: icon default must be {key}")

    scripts_dir = root / "behavior_pack/scripts"
    for jp in sorted(scripts_dir.rglob("*.js")) if scripts_dir.is_dir() else []:
        jrel = jp.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        jtxt = jp.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for spec in IMPORT_RE.findall(jtxt):
            check(spec in ALLOWED_MODULES or spec.startswith(("./", "../")),
                  f"{jrel}: import of '{spec}' is not allowed (only {', '.join(ALLOWED_MODULES)} and relative files)")
        check(not re.search(r"\beval\s*\(|\bFunction\s*\(", jtxt), f"{jrel}: eval and the Function constructor are not allowed")
        check(not re.search(r"\bimport\s*\(\s*(?![\"'])", jtxt), f"{jrel}: dynamic import with a computed specifier is not allowed (it bypasses the module allowlist)")
        if re.search(r"\b(?:globalThis|window|self)\s*\[", jtxt):
            warns.append(f"{jrel}: indexed global access can bypass the eval and import checks: a reviewer must read this file")
        if _has_comment(jtxt):
            warns.append(jrel + ": remove comments (no comments in scripts)")
        ran, err = _node_syntax(jp)
        if ran and err:
            fails.append(f"{jrel}: syntax error: {err}")
        elif not ran:
            warns.append(f"{jrel}: syntax not checked (node not found or not working)")

    if ptype == "map":
        icon_checks("trader_ledger", "allenscreations:trader_ledger", "behavior_pack/items/trader_ledger.json", LEDGER_SHA256, (32, 32))
    if controller:
        icon_checks("controller", "allenscreations:controller", "behavior_pack/items/controller.json", CONTROLLER_SHA256, (16, 16))
        mj = root / "behavior_pack/scripts/main.js"
        cj = root / CONTROLLER_JS_REL
        if mj.is_file():
            js = mj.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            if "registerController(" in js:
                check(cj.is_file(), "missing required file: " + CONTROLLER_JS_REL)
                if cj.is_file():
                    check(cj.read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("\r\n", "\n") == CONTROLLER_JS,
                          "controller.js must be the unchanged generator copy")
                check(re.search(r"from\s+[\"']\./controller\.js[\"']", js) is not None,
                      "main.js: must import registerController from ./controller.js")
                for key in ("title", "about", "gameInfo", "howToPlay"):
                    check(re.search(r"\b" + key + r"\s*:\s*(?![\"'`]{2})\S", js) is not None,
                          f"main.js: registerController needs a non-empty {key}")
                check(re.search(r"\bonStart\s*[:(]", js) is not None, "main.js: registerController needs onStart")
                mv = re.search(r"\bversion\s*:\s*[\"'](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)[\"']", js)
                check(mv is not None, "main.js: registerController needs version: \"X.Y.Z\" (the Behavior Pack version)")
                bpv = (jload("behavior_pack/manifest.json") or {}).get("header", {}).get("version")
                if mv and isinstance(bpv, list):
                    check(mv.group(1) == ".".join(str(x) for x in bpv),
                          f"main.js: registerController version {mv.group(1)} must equal the Behavior Pack manifest version")
                pj_js = root / PIXELIFY_JS_REL
                check(pj_js.is_file() and pj_js.read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("\r\n", "\n") == PIXELIFY_JS,
                      f"{PIXELIFY_JS_REL} must be the unchanged generator copy")
                if repo_root:
                    for msg in install_skin(root, repo_root):
                        check(False, msg)
                    for s, d in SKIN_FILES:
                        ref, got = Path(repo_root) / SKIN_REL / s, root / d
                        check(got.is_file() and ref.is_file() and got.read_bytes() == ref.read_bytes(),
                              f"{d} must be a byte-for-byte copy of the repo skin file")
                if "allenscreations:controller" in js:
                    warns.append("main.js mentions the controller item; controller.js already handles it")
            else:
                warns.append("main.js has an inline controller (legacy); move it to controller.js with registerController")
                check("allenscreations:controller" in js, "main.js never mentions allenscreations:controller")
                check("ActionFormData" in js, "main.js must open the controller with ActionFormData")
                btn = []
                for label in CONTROLLER_LABELS:
                    mt = re.search(r"\.button\(\s*[\"']" + label + r"[\"']", js)
                    check(mt is not None, f"main.js: controller button '{label}' not found")
                    btn.append(mt.start() if mt else None)
                if all(x is not None for x in btn):
                    check(btn == sorted(btn), "main.js: controller buttons must be added in the order Start, How to play")
                check(re.search(r"\.button\(\s*[\"'](?:Restart)[\"']", js) is None, "main.js: the controller form must not have a Restart button")
                check(re.search(r"getGameMode\(\)\s*[!=]==?\s*GameMode\.Creative", js) is not None,
                      "main.js: the controller must check GameMode.Creative with getGameMode()")
                check(re.search(r"\.setItem\(", js) is not None, "main.js: Start must consume the controller outside Creative (setItem)")
                check("scriptEventReceive" in js and "allenscreations:end" in js, "main.js: must handle the allenscreations:end script event")
            if "setInterval" in js or "system.runInterval" in js:
                warns.append("main.js uses a repeating timer; confirm it is not a per-tick scan")

    gf = root / "behavior_pack/functions/give_controller.mcfunction"
    if controller and gf.is_file():
        check(GIVE_LINE in gf.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), "give_controller.mcfunction must contain: " + GIVE_LINE)
    ef = root / "behavior_pack/functions/end_game.mcfunction"
    if controller and ef.is_file():
        check(END_LINE in ef.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), "end_game.mcfunction must contain: " + END_LINE)
    for fn in (root / "behavior_pack/functions").glob("**/*.mcfunction") if (root / "behavior_pack/functions").is_dir() else []:
        if any(ln.lstrip().startswith("#") for ln in fn.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()):
            warns.append(fn.relative_to(root).as_posix() + ": remove comments (no comments in functions)")

    readme, desc_p, chg = root / "README.md", root / "DESCRIPTION.md", root / "CHANGELOG.md"
    rtext = readme.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if readme.is_file() else ""
    dtext = desc_p.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if desc_p.is_file() else ""
    ctext = chg.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if chg.is_file() else ""
    both = (rtext + "\n" + dtext).lower()
    if rtext or dtext:
        if "not tested in game" not in both:
            warns.append("DOC: README.md or DESCRIPTION.md should state: source created, not tested in game")
        if not any(a in both for a in ACHIEVEMENT):
            warns.append("DOC: needs one achievement status line (Likely achievement-friendly / Not achievement-friendly / Unknown)")
        if "experiment" not in both:
            warns.append("DOC: needs an experiments line (for example: No experiment requirement detected in the inspected files.)")
    for url in re.findall(r"https?://[^\s)>\]]+", rtext):
        if not url.startswith((REPO_URL, REPO_URL.replace("/tree/", "/blob/"), RAW_BUILDS_URL)):
            warns.append(f"DOC: README.md link must point to the official repository: {url}")
    for name, text in (("README.md", rtext), ("DESCRIPTION.md", dtext)):
        low = text.lower()
        if "script api" in low or "@minecraft/" in low:
            warns.append(f"DOC: {name} must not mention Script API or @minecraft modules (Part 2)")
        if any(ln.lstrip().startswith("|") for ln in text.splitlines()):
            warns.append(f"DOC: {name} must not contain Markdown tables (Part 2)")
    low_r, low_d = rtext.lower(), dtext.lower()
    if re.search(r"img\.shields\.io|!\[[^\]]*\]\(", rtext):
        warns.append("DOC: README.md must not contain badges or images (Part 2)")
    if dtext and "not an official minecraft product" not in low_d:
        warns.append("DOC: DESCRIPTION.md attribution block needs the unofficial-product disclaimer (Part 2, Section 4)")
    if re.search(r"give_controller|end_game|oneblock_setup|/function\b", both) and "cheats" not in both:
        warns.append("DOC: documentation mentions an admin function or command but not that it needs cheats, which disable achievements")
    mev_doc = mevs.get("behavior_pack")
    if isinstance(mev_doc, list) and len(mev_doc) == 3 and mev_doc != [0, 0, 0]:
        for m in re.finditer(r"(\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\+?\s+or newer|Bedrock Edition:?\s*(\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?)", rtext + "\n" + dtext):
            v = [int(x) for x in (m.group(1) or m.group(2)).split(".")]
            v += [0] * (3 - len(v))
            if v != mev_doc:
                warns.append(f"DOC: documentation names Minecraft {'.'.join(map(str, v))} but min_engine_version is {'.'.join(map(str, mev_doc))}: state only the verified minimum")
    nonempty = lambda t: len([ln for ln in t.splitlines() if ln.strip()])
    if dtext and (GAMEPLAY_HEADING.search(dtext) is None or nonempty(dtext) < 25):
        page_name = "DESCRIPTION.md"
        warns.append(f"DOC: {page_name} is the product page for this type: needs an Overview/Features section and at least 25 non-empty lines")
    if dtext:
        for h in ("## Guidelines and Attribution", "## Search & Discovery Keywords"):
            if h not in dtext:
                warns.append(f"DOC: DESCRIPTION.md lacks '{h}' (Part 2)")
    if ctext:
        if re.search(r"https?://", ctext):
            warns.append("DOC: CHANGELOG.md must not contain links (Part 2)")
        if re.search(r"(?m)^## \[\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?\] - \d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", ctext) is None:
            warns.append("DOC: CHANGELOG.md needs entries like '## [1.0.0] - YYYY-MM-DD' (Part 2)")
    try:
        import doc_template as _dt
        _doc_errors = _dt.check_docs(root, ptype)
    except Exception as exc:  # a broken checker must never let documentation through silently
        _doc_errors = [f"DOC: the template checker could not run ({exc})"]
    _soft = "should state: source created, not tested in game"
    _moved = [w for w in warns if w.startswith("DOC:") and _soft not in w]
    warns[:] = [w for w in warns if w not in _moved]
    for _e in _doc_errors + _moved:
        if _e not in fails:
            fails.append(_e)
    return fails, warns, len(files), total


def pack(root, out):
    root = Path(root)
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
        for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file():
                info = zipfile.ZipInfo(p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), date_time=(2020, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0))
                info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
                info.external_attr = 0o644 << 16
                z.writestr(info, p.read_bytes(), compresslevel=9)
    data = buf.getvalue()
    out.write_text(base64.b64encode(data).decode("ascii"), encoding="ascii")  # always one line
    back = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(base64.b64decode(out.read_text(), validate=True)))
    names = set(back.namelist())
    want = {
        p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        for p in root.rglob("*")
        if p.is_file()
    }
    if back.testzip() is not None or names != want:
        print(f"ROUND-TRIP FAILED: {back.testzip()} / diff {sorted(names ^ want)}")
        sys.exit(2)
    print(f"ZIP bytes: {len(data)}   payload.b64 chars: {out.stat().st_size}   round-trip OK ({len(names)} files)")
    if len(data) > ZIP_LIMIT:
        print(f"ZIP is over {ZIP_LIMIT} bytes: do NOT paste it into the workflow. Deliver {out.name} as a file "
              "(commit to MCPEDL/incoming/<n>.b64 and use payload_file).")
    return len(data)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project", help="e.g. build/Project-Name")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="payload.b64")
    ap.add_argument("--check-only", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    root = Path(a.project)
    if not root.is_dir():
        sys.exit(f"Project folder not found: {root}")
    if read_project(root) is None:
        sys.exit('Missing or invalid project.json. Create it first: {"type": "addon|map|script|minigame", "name": "Display Name"}')
    install_assets(root)
    fails, warns, n, total = run_checks(root)
    for w in sorted(set(warns)):
        print("WARNING:", w)
    fails = [f for f in fails if f]
    if fails:
        print("SELF-TEST FAILED")
        for f in sorted(set(fails)):
            print(" -", f)
        sys.exit(1)
    print(f"SELF-TEST PASSED ({n} files, {total} bytes)")
    if not a.check_only:
        pack(root, Path(a.out))
        print("BUILD OK. Structure only: not tested in game.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Import a base64 ZIP produced by an AI agent into the repository.

Supports four project types, read from project.json inside the payload:
  addon    -> MCPEDL/Addons/<Name>/    (LOCK markers are written here)
  map      -> MCPEDL/Maps/<Name>/      (OneBlock; oneblock.json is written here)
  script   -> MCPEDL/Script/<Name>/
  minigame -> MCPEDL/Minigame/<Name>/  (Controller)

Every imported project also gets an assets/ folder (snapshots/, recipes/, items/, each with a .gitkeep) when
it is missing. Existing files in it are never touched. The build and the recipe-image workflow fill it.

Modes (--mode):
  auto      update when the project already exists, otherwise create (the workflow's default)
  create    new project only; fails if the name already exists anywhere under MCPEDL/ (default)
  update    merge the payload into the existing project   (the matching category for the project type).
            Files that are not in the payload (icons, images, LOCK) are kept; nothing is deleted.
            Pack and module UUIDs must match the existing ones and the pack version must be higher.
  overwrite replace the whole project folder with the payload (the old --overwrite flag)

Everything is validated BEFORE anything is written, using the same run_checks() the agent ran
(scripts/build_payload.py). Structural checks only: they never prove it works in Minecraft.
A legacy payload with only oneblock.json and no project.json is treated as a map.

Usage:
  python scripts/import_project_payload.py [--name My-Project] [--mode auto|create|update|overwrite]
      [--payload-file my-project.b64] [--root DIR] [--github-output FILE]
Payload source, in this order: env PROJECT_PAYLOAD (or ONEBLOCK_PAYLOAD) when --payload-file is empty,
else --payload-file (a bare file name is looked up in MCPEDL/incoming/), else the only .b64 file in
MCPEDL/incoming/. When --name is empty it is taken from the name in the payload's project.json (Monster-Companions.b64 ->
Monster-Companions).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import base64
import binascii
import io
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import stat
import sys
import tempfile
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import build_payload as bp  # noqa: E402  (single source of truth for the checks)

DEST = bp.DEST_DIR
ALL_DIRS = ("Addons", "Maps", "Script", "Minigame")
COMPAT = {"addon": ("Addons",), "map": ("Maps",), "script": ("Script",), "minigame": ("Minigame",)}
MODES = ("auto", "create", "update", "overwrite")
PACKS = ("behavior_pack", "resource_pack")
ASSET_FOLDERS = ("snapshots", "recipes", "items")
MAX_B64 = 36_000_000
CONTROL = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f]")
LOCK_ROOT = ("ALNStudios Add-on Lock v1\n\nRequired repository marker for build validation.\n"
             "This marker does not encrypt source files or prevent extraction of distributable packages.\n")
LOCK_PACK = ("ALNStudios Add-on Lock v1\n\nPack marker included in the exported Minecraft Bedrock pack.\n"
             "This marker does not encrypt the pack or prevent extraction or editing.\n")


def fail(msg: str) -> None:
    sys.exit(msg)


SH_BEGIN = "#--- ALN PAYLOAD BEGIN ---"
SH_END = "#--- ALN PAYLOAD END ---"


def payload_from_text(text: str, name: str = "") -> str:
    """A pack script (.sh) carries its base64 between two marker lines, each payload line starting with '#'.
    The file is only read, never executed. Any other text is returned unchanged."""
    lines = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n")
    if SH_BEGIN not in lines:
        if name.lower().endswith(".sh"):
            fail(f"{name} has no payload block ({SH_BEGIN} ... {SH_END}).")
        return text
    start = lines.index(SH_BEGIN) + 1
    if SH_END not in lines[start:]:
        fail(f"{name or 'pack script'}: the payload block is not closed ({SH_END}).")
    end = lines.index(SH_END, start)
    return "".join(l.lstrip("#").strip() for l in lines[start:end])


def resolve_payload(root: Path, payload_file: str | None) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
    """Return (base64 text, repo-relative payload path or None when the text came from the environment)."""
    incoming = (root / "MCPEDL" / "incoming").resolve()
    env_text = os.environ.get("PROJECT_PAYLOAD") or os.environ.get("ONEBLOCK_PAYLOAD", "")
    path: Path | None = None
    if payload_file:
        given = Path(payload_file.strip())
        path = (incoming / given.name) if len(given.parts) == 1 else (root / given).resolve()
        if not path.is_file() and not path.suffix:
            # The extension is optional: "Monster-Companions" finds Monster-Companions.b64 (or .txt).
            for ext in (".b64", ".txt", ".sh"):
                if path.with_name(path.name + ext).is_file():
                    path = path.with_name(path.name + ext)
                    break
        try:
            path.relative_to(incoming)
        except ValueError:
            fail(f"--payload-file must be inside MCPEDL/incoming/ (got {payload_file})")
        if not path.is_file():
            fail(f"Payload file not found: {path.relative_to(root).as_posix()}")
    elif not env_text.strip():
        # .b64, .txt, or no extension at all; dotfiles and READMEs are ignored.
        found = sorted(f for f in incoming.iterdir()
                       if f.is_file() and not f.name.startswith(".") and f.suffix.lower() in (".b64", ".txt", ".sh", "")
                       and f.stem.lower() != "readme") if incoming.is_dir() else []
        if not found:
            fail("No payload: commit a payload file (.b64, .txt, or no extension) to MCPEDL/incoming/ or paste base64 into the workflow.")
        if len(found) > 1:
            fail("Several payload files in MCPEDL/incoming/: " + ", ".join(f.name for f in found)
                 + ". Enter the file name in payload_file.")
        path = found[0]
    if path is not None:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        rel = path.resolve().relative_to(root).as_posix()
        text = payload_from_text(text, path.name)
    else:
        text, rel = env_text, None
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", "", text)
    if not text:
        fail("Empty payload. Provide base64 text or a payload file.")
    if len(text) > MAX_B64:
        fail(f"Payload is {len(text)} base64 characters (max {MAX_B64}).")
    return text, rel


def decode_zip(b64: str) -> zipfile.ZipFile:
    try:
        raw = base64.b64decode(b64, validate=True)
    except (binascii.Error, ValueError) as exc:
        fail(f"Payload is not valid base64: {exc}")
    if not zipfile.is_zipfile(io.BytesIO(raw)):
        fail("Decoded payload is not a ZIP archive.")
    return zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw))


def collect_files(z: zipfile.ZipFile, errors: list[str]) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    infos = [i for i in z.infolist() if not i.filename.endswith("/")]
    if not infos:
        fail("ZIP contains no files.")
    if len(infos) > bp.MAX_FILES:
        fail(f"ZIP has {len(infos)} files (max {bp.MAX_FILES}).")
    declared = sum(i.file_size for i in infos)
    if declared > bp.MAX_TOTAL:
        fail(f"Declared uncompressed size {declared} exceeds {bp.MAX_TOTAL} bytes. Nothing was read.")
    names, seen = [], {}
    for info in infos:
        name = info.filename
        parts = PurePosixPath(name).parts
        if "\\" in name or name.startswith("/") or ".." in parts or any(":" in p for p in parts) or CONTROL.search(name):
            errors.append(f"Unsafe path in ZIP: {name!r}")
        if stat.S_ISLNK(info.external_attr >> 16):
            errors.append(f"Symlink not allowed: {name!r}")
        key = name.lower()
        if key in seen:
            errors.append(f"Duplicate or case-colliding ZIP entry: {name!r} and {seen[key]!r}")
        seen[key] = name
        names.append(name)
    prefix = ""
    if "project.json" not in names and "oneblock.json" not in names:
        tops = {PurePosixPath(n).parts[0] for n in names}
        if len(tops) == 1 and all(len(PurePosixPath(n).parts) > 1 for n in names):
            prefix = next(iter(tops)) + "/"
    files: dict[str, bytes] = {}
    total = 0
    for info in infos:
        rel = info.filename[len(prefix):] if prefix else info.filename
        if info.file_size > bp.MAX_FILE:
            errors.append(f"File too large: {rel} ({info.file_size} bytes)")
            continue
        with z.open(info) as fh:
            data = fh.read(bp.MAX_FILE + 1)
        if len(data) > bp.MAX_FILE:
            errors.append(f"File too large: {rel} (more than {bp.MAX_FILE} bytes)")
            continue
        total += len(data)
        if total > bp.MAX_TOTAL:
            fail(f"Total uncompressed size exceeds {bp.MAX_TOTAL} bytes. Stopped reading.")
        files[rel] = data
    return files


def legacy_to_project(files: dict[str, bytes], errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Old payloads had only oneblock.json: treat them as maps."""
    if "project.json" in files or "oneblock.json" not in files:
        return
    try:
        cfg = json.loads(files["oneblock.json"].decode("utf-8-sig"))
        files["project.json"] = (json.dumps({"type": "map", "name": cfg["name"]}, indent=2) + "\n").encode()
        print("::warning::Legacy payload (oneblock.json only): treated as type 'map'.")
    except (ValueError, KeyError, UnicodeDecodeError):
        errors.append("oneblock.json is not valid JSON with a 'name'")


def name_from_project_json(files: dict[str, bytes]) -> str:
    """Folder name = project.json 'name', spaces to hyphens, other unsafe characters removed."""
    try:
        display = json.loads(files["project.json"].decode("utf-8-sig"))["name"]
    except (KeyError, ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError):
        fail("project.json is missing or has no 'name'. The name must be inside the payload.")
    if not isinstance(display, str):
        fail("project.json 'name' must be a string.")
    slug = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "", re.sub(r"\s+", "-", display.strip())).strip("-._")
    return slug[:64]


def find_projects(root: Path, name: str) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, Path]], list[tuple[str, Path]]]:
    """Return (exact-name matches, same-name-different-case matches) across every MCPEDL project folder."""
    exact, loose = [], []
    for d in ALL_DIRS:
        folder = root / "MCPEDL" / d
        if not folder.is_dir():
            continue
        for c in sorted(folder.iterdir()):
            if not c.is_dir() or c.name.endswith(".importing"):
                continue
            if c.name == name:
                exact.append((d, c))
            elif c.name.lower() == name.lower():
                loose.append((d, c))
    return exact, loose


def read_manifest(path: Path) -> dict | None:
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def check_identity(stage: Path, dest: Path, mode: str, errors: list[str], warns: list[str]) -> None:
    """update: UUIDs must match and the version must go up. overwrite: only warn about changed UUIDs."""
    strict = mode == "update"
    for label in PACKS:
        new = read_manifest(stage / label / "manifest.json")
        old = read_manifest(dest / label / "manifest.json")
        if old and not new:
            warns.append(f"{label} exists in the project but not in the payload: it is kept as is" if strict
                         else f"{label} exists in the project but not in the payload: it will be deleted")
            continue
        if not (new and old):
            continue
        nh, oh = new.get("header") or {}, old.get("header") or {}
        n_mods = {m.get("uuid") for m in new.get("modules") or []}
        o_mods = {m.get("uuid") for m in old.get("modules") or []}
        if nh.get("uuid") != oh.get("uuid") or n_mods != o_mods:
            msg = (f"{label}: pack or module UUIDs differ from the existing project. Worlds that already use the pack "
                   "would treat it as a different pack. Keep the existing UUIDs.")
            (errors if strict else warns).append(msg)
        if strict:
            nv, ov = nh.get("version"), oh.get("version")
            if isinstance(nv, list) and isinstance(ov, list) and tuple(nv) <= tuple(ov):
                errors.append(f"{label}: version {'.'.join(map(str, nv))} is not higher than the existing "
                              f"{'.'.join(map(str, ov))}. Bump the version before updating.")


def ensure_assets(dest: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Create <dest>/assets/{snapshots,recipes,items}/.gitkeep where missing. Never touches existing files."""
    made = []
    for kind in ASSET_FOLDERS:
        keep = dest / "assets" / kind / ".gitkeep"
        has_files = (dest / "assets" / kind).is_dir() and any((dest / "assets" / kind).iterdir())
        if not has_files and not keep.exists():
            keep.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            keep.touch()
            made.append(f"assets/{kind}")
    return made


def merge_into(stage: Path, dest: Path, ptype: str) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], int, int]:
    """Copy payload files over the existing project. Never deletes. Returns added, changed, unchanged, kept."""
    staged = {p.relative_to(stage).as_posix(): p for p in stage.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    kept = sum(1 for p in dest.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and p.relative_to(dest).as_posix() not in staged)
    added, changed, same = [], [], 0
    for rel, p in sorted(staged.items()):
        target = dest / rel
        if ptype == "addon" and PurePosixPath(rel).name == "LOCK" and target.exists():
            same += 1
            continue
        data = p.read_bytes()
        if target.exists():
            if target.read_bytes() == data:
                same += 1
                continue
            changed.append(rel)
        else:
            added.append(rel)
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_bytes(data)
    return added, changed, same, kept


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--name", "--map", dest="name", default="", help="Folder name (default: the .b64 file name)")
    ap.add_argument("--mode", choices=MODES, help="create (default), auto, update, or overwrite")
    ap.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true", help="Same as --mode overwrite (kept for older callers)")
    ap.add_argument("--payload-file")
    ap.add_argument("--root", default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
    ap.add_argument("--github-output")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    mode = args.mode or ("overwrite" if args.overwrite else "create")
    root = Path(args.root).resolve()

    errors: list[str] = []
    b64, payload_rel = resolve_payload(root, args.payload_file)
    files = collect_files(decode_zip(b64), errors)
    legacy_to_project(files, errors)
    if not args.name:
        args.name = name_from_project_json(files)
        print(f"Project name taken from project.json: {args.name}")
    if not bp.NAME_RE.match(args.name):
        fail("Name: 1-64 chars, letters/numbers/. _ - only, starting with a letter or number.")

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="payload-") as tmp:
        stage = Path(tmp) / "p"
        stage.mkdir()
        for rel, data in files.items():
            if ".." in PurePosixPath(rel).parts or rel.startswith("/"):
                continue  # already reported
            t = stage / rel
            t.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            t.write_bytes(data)
        bp.install_skin(stage, root)
        fails, warns, _, _ = bp.run_checks(stage, repo_root=root, folder=args.name)
        pj = bp.read_project(stage) or {}
        ptype = pj.get("type")
        if ptype not in bp.TYPES:
            for e in sorted(set(errors + [f for f in fails if f])):
                print(f"::error::{e}")
            fail("Import rejected: project.json type must be addon, map, script, or minigame. Nothing was written.")

        exact, loose = find_projects(root, args.name)
        compat = [(d, c) for d, c in exact if d in COMPAT[ptype]]
        foreign = [(d, c) for d, c in exact if d not in COMPAT[ptype]]
        existing: Path | None = None
        if mode == "auto":
            mode = "update" if compat else "create"
            print(f"Mode auto -> {mode} ({'found' if compat else 'no existing'} {ptype} named {args.name})")
        if mode == "create":
            if exact or loose:
                fail("Name clash: " + ", ".join(f"MCPEDL/{d}/{c.name}" for d, c in exact + loose)
                     + " already exists. Use import_mode update to change it, overwrite to replace it, or pick another name.")
        else:
            if len(compat) > 1:
                fail("Ambiguous: " + ", ".join(f"MCPEDL/{d}/{c.name}" for d, c in compat)
                     + " all exist. Remove the duplicate first.")
            if compat:
                existing = compat[0][1]
            if mode == "update":
                if loose:
                    fail("Name differs only by letter case from " + ", ".join(f"MCPEDL/{d}/{c.name}" for d, c in loose)
                         + ". Use the exact folder name.")
                if existing is None:
                    where = (" It exists as another project type: "
                             + ", ".join(f"MCPEDL/{d}/{c.name}" for d, c in foreign)) if foreign else ""
                    fail(f"Nothing to update: no {ptype} named {args.name} under MCPEDL/." + where + " Use import_mode create for a new project.")
            else:
                for d, c in foreign + loose:
                    print(f"::warning::overwrite enabled: MCPEDL/{d}/{c.name} also exists and is left untouched")

        dest = existing if existing is not None else root / "MCPEDL" / DEST[ptype] / args.name
        if existing is not None:
            own = f"MCPEDL/{existing.parent.name}/{existing.name}:"
            fails = [f for f in fails if f and not (f.startswith("UUID ") and "is already used by " + own in f)]
        errors += [f for f in fails if f]
        if existing is not None:
            check_identity(stage, existing, mode, errors, warns)
        for w in sorted(set(warns)):
            print(f"::warning::{w}")
        if errors:
            for e in sorted(set(errors)):
                print(f"::error::{e}")
            fail(f"Import rejected: {len(set(errors))} error(s). Nothing was written.")

        if ptype == "map":
            (stage / "oneblock.json").write_text(
                json.dumps({"enabled": True, "name": pj["name"]}, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        if ptype == "addon":
            (stage / "LOCK").write_text(LOCK_ROOT, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
            for pack in PACKS:
                if (stage / pack / "manifest.json").is_file():
                    (stage / pack / "LOCK").write_text(LOCK_PACK, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")

        if mode == "update":
            added, changed, same, kept = merge_into(stage, dest, ptype)
            print(f"Update of {dest.relative_to(root)}: {len(added)} added, {len(changed)} changed, "
                  f"{same} unchanged, {kept} existing file(s) kept (not in the payload, not deleted).")
            for rel in added:
                print(f"  added    {rel}")
            for rel in changed:
                print(f"  changed  {rel}")
        else:
            if dest.exists():
                old_files = sorted(p.relative_to(dest).as_posix() for p in dest.rglob("*") if p.is_file())
                print(f"::warning::overwrite will delete {len(old_files)} existing file(s) in {dest.relative_to(root)}: "
                      + ", ".join(old_files[:20]) + (" ..." if len(old_files) > 20 else ""))
            dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            staging = dest.parent / (args.name + ".importing")
            if staging.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(staging)
            shutil.copytree(stage, staging)
            if dest.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(dest)  # replace the whole folder so no stale files remain
            staging.rename(dest)

    made = ensure_assets(dest)
    if made:
        print("Created asset folder(s): " + ", ".join(f"{dest.relative_to(root).as_posix()}/{m}" for m in made))
    print(f"Imported {len(files)} file(s) as {ptype} into {dest.relative_to(root)} (mode: {mode})")
    print("Structural validation passed. This does not prove it works in Minecraft.")
    if args.github_output:
        with open(args.github_output, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(f"type={ptype}\ndest={dest.relative_to(root).as_posix()}\nname={args.name}\n"
                     f"mode={mode}\npayload_path={payload_rel or ''}\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## FILE: scripts/crafting_template.py (206 lines, 8527 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Canonical renderer for the sole crafting-board source: scripts/CRAFTING_TABLE.svg.

The board image, every slot position, the output slot, and every board color come
from that SVG. No raster board, screenshot, or alternate GUI is ever accepted as
input. Generated PNGs are outputs only; they are not design references.

Expected SVG ids: slot-0 .. slot-8 (row-major 3x3 groups), slot-N-fill (item area),
output (group), output-fill (item area).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import lru_cache
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

import cairosvg
from PIL import Image

CRAFTING_TABLE_SVG = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "CRAFTING_TABLE.svg"
NATIVE_SIZE = (400, 222)

SLOT_ICON_SIZE = 32
OUTPUT_ICON_SIZE = 38

Rect = tuple[float, float, float, float]  # x, y, width, height in SVG pixels


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BoardLayout:
    slots: tuple[Rect, ...]  # nine item areas, row-major
    output: Rect  # output item area


def _read_svg() -> bytes:
    if not CRAFTING_TABLE_SVG.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Required sole crafting-board reference is missing: {CRAFTING_TABLE_SVG}. "
            "No alternate image or raster fallback is permitted."
        )
    return CRAFTING_TABLE_SVG.read_bytes()


def _parse_svg(source: bytes) -> ET.Element:
    try:
        return ET.fromstring(source)
    except ET.ParseError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"Unable to parse the sole crafting-board SVG: {CRAFTING_TABLE_SVG}") from exc


def _translate(element: ET.Element) -> tuple[float, float]:
    match = re.search(
        r"translate\(\s*(-?[0-9.]+)[\s,]+(-?[0-9.]+)\s*\)", element.attrib.get("transform", "")
    )
    return (float(match.group(1)), float(match.group(2))) if match else (0.0, 0.0)


def _fill_rect(group: ET.Element, fill_id: str) -> Rect:
    """Absolute rectangle of the item area `fill_id` inside `group`."""
    gx, gy = _translate(group)
    for child in group.iter():
        if child.attrib.get("id") == fill_id:
            try:
                return (
                    gx + float(child.attrib.get("x", 0)),
                    gy + float(child.attrib.get("y", 0)),
                    float(child.attrib["width"]),
                    float(child.attrib["height"]),
                )
            except (KeyError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"Element {fill_id!r} in CRAFTING_TABLE.svg needs numeric width and height") from exc
    raise ValueError(f"CRAFTING_TABLE.svg is missing required element id {fill_id!r}")


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def load_board_layout() -> BoardLayout:
    """Read slot and output positions directly from the SVG's ids."""
    root = _parse_svg(_read_svg())
    groups = {el.attrib["id"]: el for el in root.iter() if "id" in el.attrib}

    slots = []
    for index in range(9):
        group = groups.get(f"slot-{index}")
        if group is None:
            raise ValueError(f"CRAFTING_TABLE.svg is missing required element id 'slot-{index}'")
        slots.append(_fill_rect(group, f"slot-{index}-fill"))

    output_group = groups.get("output")
    if output_group is None:
        raise ValueError("CRAFTING_TABLE.svg is missing required element id 'output'")
    return BoardLayout(slots=tuple(slots), output=_fill_rect(output_group, "output-fill"))


def render_crafting_table_svg() -> Image.Image:
    """Render the exact source SVG at its native dimensions and return RGBA pixels."""
    source = _read_svg()
    root = _parse_svg(source)

    def dimension(value: str) -> float:
        match = re.fullmatch(r"\s*([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)(?:px)?\s*", value)
        if not match:
            raise ValueError(
                "CRAFTING_TABLE.svg must declare numeric width and height so its native "
                "proportions can be preserved."
            )
        return float(match.group(1))

    width, height = dimension(root.attrib.get("width", "")), dimension(root.attrib.get("height", ""))
    if width <= 0 or height <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid CRAFTING_TABLE.svg dimensions: {width} x {height}")

    expected = (round(width), round(height))
    png = cairosvg.svg2png(
        bytestring=source,
        output_width=expected[0],
        output_height=expected[1],
    )
    image = Image.open(BytesIO(png)).convert("RGBA")
    if image.size != expected:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Rendered CRAFTING_TABLE.svg dimensions {image.size} do not match "
            f"its declared native dimensions {expected}."
        )
    return image


def _centered(rect: Rect, size: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    x, y, w, h = rect
    return round(x + (w - size) / 2), round(y + (h - size) / 2)


def compose_recipe_diagram(recipe_type: str, recipe_data: dict, ingredient_icon, result_icon: Image.Image, output_count: int = 1, scale: int = 1) -> Image.Image:
    """Overlay recipe icons onto the real 3x3 grid in CRAFTING_TABLE.svg.

    Shaped recipes retain their exact pattern positions. Shapeless ingredients
    fill grid cells in row-major order. The SVG-rendered board is never rebuilt,
    and all positions and colors come from the SVG.
    """
    board = render_crafting_table_svg()
    if board.size != NATIVE_SIZE:
        raise ValueError(f"Expected native {NATIVE_SIZE[0]}x{NATIVE_SIZE[1]} crafting SVG, got {board.size}")
    layout = load_board_layout()

    grid = [[None for _ in range(3)] for _ in range(3)]
    if recipe_type == "minecraft:recipe_shaped":
        pattern = recipe_data.get("pattern", [])
        key = recipe_data.get("key", {})
        if len(pattern) > 3 or any(len(row) > 3 for row in pattern):
            raise ValueError("Shaped recipe pattern exceeds the 3x3 crafting grid")
        for row, pattern_row in enumerate(pattern):
            for col, symbol in enumerate(pattern_row):
                if symbol == " ":
                    continue
                if symbol not in key or not key[symbol].get("item"):
                    raise ValueError(f"Missing item mapping for shaped recipe symbol {symbol!r}")
                grid[row][col] = key[symbol]["item"]
    elif recipe_type == "minecraft:recipe_shapeless":
        ingredients = recipe_data.get("ingredients", [])
        if len(ingredients) > 9:
            raise ValueError(f"Shapeless recipe has {len(ingredients)} ingredients; crafting grid holds 9")
        for index, entry in enumerate(ingredients):
            item_id = entry.get("item") if isinstance(entry, dict) else None
            if not item_id:
                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported shapeless ingredient entry: {entry!r}")
            grid[index // 3][index % 3] = item_id
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported recipe type: {recipe_type}")

    for row in range(3):
        for col in range(3):
            item_id = grid[row][col]
            if item_id is None:
                continue
            icon = ingredient_icon(item_id).convert("RGBA").resize(
                (SLOT_ICON_SIZE, SLOT_ICON_SIZE), Image.Resampling.NEAREST
            )
            board.alpha_composite(icon, _centered(layout.slots[row * 3 + col], SLOT_ICON_SIZE))

    result = result_icon.convert("RGBA").resize((OUTPUT_ICON_SIZE, OUTPUT_ICON_SIZE), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    board.alpha_composite(result, _centered(layout.output, OUTPUT_ICON_SIZE))

    if output_count > 1:
        try:
            from PIL import ImageDraw, ImageFont

            ox, oy, ow, oh = layout.output
            right, bottom = round(ox + ow) - 2, round(oy + oh) - 4
            left, top = right - 14, bottom - 13
            # Badge background uses the slot color sampled from the SVG render.
            badge_fill = board.getpixel((round(ox) + 1, round(oy) + 1))

            draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)
            font_path = Path("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf")
            font = ImageFont.truetype(str(font_path), 10) if font_path.is_file() else ImageFont.load_default()
            draw.rectangle((left, top, right, bottom), fill=badge_fill)
            draw.text((left + 2, top), str(output_count), font=font, fill=(255, 255, 255, 255), stroke_width=1, stroke_fill=(35, 35, 35, 255))
        except Exception as exc:
            raise RuntimeError("Unable to render recipe output count") from exc

    if scale != 1:
        board = board.resize((board.width * scale, board.height * scale), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    return board
```
