#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate crafting recipe diagrams for ANY add-on pack (general generator, not tied to one pack).

For every pack under MCPEDL/*/* that has behavior_pack/recipes/*.json, each shaped or shapeless crafting-table
recipe becomes a 400x222 diagram at <pack>/assets/recipes/<result>_recipe.png, drawn on scripts/CRAFTING_TABLE.svg by
scripts/crafting_template.py. The description builder shows that folder automatically.

Textures
  custom items  : the pack's own resource_pack (BP item icon -> item_texture.json, then textures/items/**/<name>.png)
  vanilla items : Mojang Bedrock Samples (env BEDROCK_SAMPLES_ROOT, default /tmp/bedrock-samples), through the verified
                  VANILLA_OVERRIDES table first, then textures/items/<name>.png, then Samples' item_texture.json.
                  Nothing is guessed: an ingredient with no verified texture is reported and nothing is written.
Every problem of every recipe is reported together (INSTRUCTIONS.md 1.5), and nothing is written until all resolve.

Usage
  python3 scripts/generate_recipe_images.py                  all packs (same as --all)
  python3 scripts/generate_recipe_images.py --project MCPEDL/Addons/<pack>
  python3 scripts/generate_recipe_images.py --samples PATH   use another Bedrock Samples folder
  python3 scripts/generate_recipe_images.py --check          resolve everything, write nothing (exit 1 on problems)
  python3 scripts/generate_recipe_images.py --sheet          also write assets/all_recipes_sheet.png (kept up to date
                                                             automatically when that file already exists)
  python3 scripts/generate_recipe_images.py --project MCPEDL/Addons/<pack> --item custom:my_item [--item ...]
                                                             copy item sprites to <pack>/assets/items/ (128x128)
  python3 scripts/generate_recipe_images.py --all --items-from-recipes
                                                             also copy the sprite of every custom item that has a recipe
  --include-dedicated   also process packs that still have their own generator script (skipped by default)
"""

class Unresolved(Exception):
    pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- JSON helpers
def load_json(path: Path):
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        stripped = "\n".join(l for l in text.splitlines() if not l.lstrip().startswith("//"))
        return json.loads(stripped)


def short(item_id: str) -> str:
    return item_id.split(":")[-1] if item_id.count(":") <= 1 else item_id.split(":")[1]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- vanilla textures
def _samples_rp(samples_root: Path) -> Path:
    return samples_root / "resource_pack"


def _shade(img: Image.Image, factor: float) -> Image.Image:
    img = img.copy()
    px = img.load()
    for y in range(img.height):
        for x in range(img.width):
            r, g, b, a = px[x, y]
            px[x, y] = (min(255, int(r * factor)), min(255, int(g * factor)), min(255, int(b * factor)), a)
    return img


def make_block_item_icon(face_path: Path) -> Image.Image:
    face = Image.open(face_path).convert("RGBA").resize((16, 16), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    out = Image.new("RGBA", (16, 16), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    out.paste(face.resize((12, 12), Image.Resampling.NEAREST), (2, 2))
    for x in range(2, 14):
        r, g, b, a = out.getpixel((x, 2))
        if a > 0:
            out.putpixel((x, 2), (min(255, int(r * 1.25)), min(255, int(g * 1.25)), min(255, int(b * 1.25)), a))
        r, g, b, a = out.getpixel((x, 13))
        if a > 0:
            out.putpixel((x, 13), (int(r * 0.65), int(g * 0.65), int(b * 0.65), a))
    for y in range(2, 14):
        r, g, b, a = out.getpixel((2, y))
        if a > 0:
            out.putpixel((2, y), (min(255, int(r * 1.15)), min(255, int(g * 1.15)), min(255, int(b * 1.15)), a))
        r, g, b, a = out.getpixel((13, y))
        if a > 0:
            out.putpixel((13, y), (int(r * 0.75), int(g * 0.75), int(b * 0.75), a))
    return out


def isometric_block_icon(side_path: Path, top_path: Path, right_shade: float = 0.72) -> Image.Image:
    icon = Image.new("RGBA", (32, 32), (0, 0, 0, 0))

    def face(texture_path, box, points, shade=1.0):
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = box
        tex = Image.open(texture_path).convert("RGBA").resize((x1 - x0, y1 - y0), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
        if shade != 1.0:
            tex = _shade(tex, shade)
        layer = Image.new("RGBA", (32, 32), (0, 0, 0, 0))
        layer.alpha_composite(tex, (x0, y0))
        mask = Image.new("L", (32, 32), 0)
        ImageDraw.Draw(mask).polygon(points, fill=255)
        layer.putalpha(ImageChops.multiply(layer.getchannel("A"), mask))
        icon.alpha_composite(layer)

    face(top_path, (4, 2, 28, 16), [(16, 2), (28, 9), (16, 16), (4, 9)])
    face(side_path, (4, 9, 17, 30), [(4, 9), (16, 16), (16, 30), (4, 23)])
    face(side_path, (16, 9, 29, 30), [(16, 16), (28, 9), (28, 23), (16, 30)], right_shade)
    return icon


def _texture_from_json(rp: Path, name: str) -> Path | None:
    """item_texture.json: texture_data[name].textures -> <rp>/<path>.png"""
    index = rp / "textures" / "item_texture.json"
    if not index.is_file():
        return None
    entry = load_json(index).get("texture_data", {}).get(name)
    if not entry:
        return None
    tex = entry.get("textures")
    if isinstance(tex, list):
        tex = tex[0] if tex else None
    if isinstance(tex, dict):
        tex = tex.get("path")
    if not isinstance(tex, str):
        return None
    path = rp / (tex if tex.endswith(".png") else tex + ".png")
    return path if path.is_file() else None


def vanilla_icon(item_id: str, samples_root: Path = None) -> Image.Image:
    rp = _samples_rp(samples_root or SAMPLES_ROOT)
    name = short(item_id)
    entry = VANILLA_OVERRIDES.get(f"minecraft:{name}")
    if entry:
        path, kind = rp / entry[0], entry[1]
        if not path.is_file():
            raise Unresolved(f"{item_id}: mapped Samples texture is missing: {path}")
        if kind == "block_item":
            return make_block_item_icon(path)
        if kind == "iso_block":
            top = rp / entry[2]
            if not top.is_file():
                raise Unresolved(f"{item_id}: Samples top texture is missing: {top}")
            return isometric_block_icon(path, top)
        if kind == "head_item":
            head = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").crop((0, 0, 16, 16))
            out = Image.new("RGBA", (16, 16), (0, 0, 0, 0))
            out.paste(head.resize((12, 12), Image.Resampling.NEAREST), (2, 2))
            return out
        im = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA")  # item, plant_item, flat_block
        return im if im.size == (16, 16) else im.resize((16, 16), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    direct = rp / "textures" / "items" / f"{name}.png"
    if direct.is_file():
        return Image.open(direct).convert("RGBA")
    indexed = _texture_from_json(rp, name)
    if indexed:
        return Image.open(indexed).convert("RGBA")
    raise Unresolved(f"{item_id}: no verified vanilla texture. Add it to VANILLA_OVERRIDES after checking Samples "
                     "(never guess a block texture).")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- custom textures (the pack's own files)
def custom_texture_path(pack: Path, item_id: str) -> Path | None:
    rp = pack / "resource_pack"
    name = short(item_id)
    icon_name = None
    items_dir = pack / "behavior_pack" / "items"
    if items_dir.is_dir():
        for f in sorted(items_dir.rglob("*.json")):
            try:
                data = load_json(f).get("minecraft:item", {})
            except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
                continue
            if data.get("description", {}).get("identifier") != item_id:
                continue
            icon = data.get("components", {}).get("minecraft:icon")
            if isinstance(icon, str):
                icon_name = icon
            elif isinstance(icon, dict):
                tex = icon.get("texture") or (icon.get("textures") or {}).get("default")
                icon_name = tex if isinstance(tex, str) else None
            break
    for candidate in (icon_name, name):
        if candidate:
            found = _texture_from_json(rp, candidate)
            if found:
                return found
    hits = sorted((rp / "textures" / "items").rglob(f"{name}.png")) if (rp / "textures" / "items").is_dir() else []
    return hits[0] if hits else None


def icon_for(pack: Path, item_id: str, samples_root: Path = None) -> Image.Image:
    if item_id.startswith("minecraft:") or ":" not in item_id:
        return vanilla_icon(item_id, samples_root)
    path = custom_texture_path(pack, item_id)
    if not path:
        raise Unresolved(f"{item_id}: no texture found in the pack's resource_pack (checked the BP item icon, "
                         "item_texture.json and textures/items/**).")
    return Image.open(path).convert("RGBA")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- recipes
def recipe_files(pack: Path) -> list[Path]:
    d = pack / "behavior_pack" / "recipes"
    return sorted(d.rglob("*.json")) if d.is_dir() else []


def parse_recipe(path: Path):
    """-> (type, data) for a crafting-table recipe, or None when it is another kind of recipe."""
    doc = load_json(path)
    for kind in SUPPORTED:
        if kind in doc:
            data = doc[kind]
            tags = data.get("tags")
            if tags is not None and "crafting_table" not in tags:
                return None
            return kind, data
    return None


def result_of(data: dict) -> tuple[str, int]:
    res = data.get("result")
    if isinstance(res, str):
        return res, 1
    if isinstance(res, dict) and res.get("item"):
        return res["item"], int(res.get("count", 1))
    raise Unresolved("recipe has no usable result item")


def ingredient_entries(kind: str, data: dict):
    if kind == "minecraft:recipe_shaped":
        used = {c for row in data.get("pattern", []) for c in row if c != " "}
        return [data.get("key", {}).get(c) for c in sorted(used)]
    return list(data.get("ingredients", []))


def plan_pack(pack: Path, samples_root: Path = None):
    """Resolve every recipe of a pack. -> (jobs, skipped, problems). Nothing is written here."""
    jobs, skipped, problems = [], [], []
    names_used = {}
    for f in recipe_files(pack):
        label = f.relative_to(pack).as_posix()
        try:
            parsed = parse_recipe(f)
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
            problems.append(f"{label}: cannot read recipe ({exc})")
            continue
        if parsed is None:
            skipped.append(label)
            continue
        kind, data = parsed
        try:
            result_id, count = result_of(data)
            for entry in ingredient_entries(kind, data):
                if not isinstance(entry, dict) or not entry.get("item"):
                    raise Unresolved(f"ingredient {entry!r} has no single item (tag or missing key); add an explicit item")
                if entry.get("data") not in (None, 0):
                    raise Unresolved(f"{entry['item']} uses data value {entry['data']}; one icon cannot show it")
            ingredients = {e["item"] for e in ingredient_entries(kind, data)}
            icons = {i: icon_for(pack, i, samples_root) for i in sorted(ingredients)}
            result_icon = icon_for(pack, result_id, samples_root)
        except Unresolved as exc:
            problems.append(f"{label}: {exc}")
            continue
        slug = short(result_id)
        out_name = f"{slug}_recipe.png"
        if out_name in names_used:
            out_name = f"{slug}_{f.stem}_recipe.png"
        names_used[out_name] = f
        jobs.append(dict(file=f, kind=kind, data=data, count=count, icons=icons, result=result_icon, result_id=result_id, out=out_name))
    return jobs, skipped, problems


def write_pack(pack: Path, jobs: list, with_sheet: bool):
    from crafting_template import compose_recipe_diagram  # needs CairoSVG; imported only when writing
    out_dir = pack / "assets" / "recipes"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    written = []
    for job in jobs:
        icons = job["icons"]
        diagram = compose_recipe_diagram(job["kind"], job["data"], lambda i, _m=icons: _m[i], job["result"], job["count"], scale=1)
        target = out_dir / job["out"]
        diagram.save(target)
        written.append(target)
    sheet = pack / "assets" / "all_recipes_sheet.png"
    if written and (with_sheet or sheet.is_file()):
        pad, w, h = 8, 400, 222
        canvas = Image.new("RGBA", (w + 2 * pad, len(written) * h + (len(written) + 1) * pad), (35, 35, 38, 255))
        for i, p in enumerate(sorted(written)):
            with Image.open(p) as frame:
                canvas.paste(frame, (pad, pad + i * (h + pad)))
        canvas.save(sheet)
        written.append(sheet)
    return written


def export_items(pack: Path, item_ids: list[str], samples_root: Path = None):
    """Copy item sprites to <pack>/assets/items/<name>.png, nearest-neighbor ITEM_SPRITE_SIZE square."""
    out_dir = pack / "assets" / "items"
    problems, written = [], []
    for item_id in item_ids:
        try:
            icon = icon_for(pack, item_id, samples_root)
        except Unresolved as exc:
            problems.append(str(exc))
            continue
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target = out_dir / f"{short(item_id)}.png"
        icon.resize((ITEM_SPRITE_SIZE, ITEM_SPRITE_SIZE), Image.Resampling.NEAREST).save(target)
        written.append(target)
    return written, problems


def custom_result_ids(jobs: list) -> list[str]:
    """Custom (non-vanilla) result items of the planned recipes, in order, without duplicates."""
    seen, out = set(), []
    for job in jobs:
        rid = job["result_id"]
        if ":" in rid and not rid.startswith("minecraft:") and rid not in seen:
            seen.add(rid)
            out.append(rid)
    return out


def find_packs(root: Path, project: str | None, include_dedicated: bool):
    if project:
        p = Path(project)
        p = p if p.is_absolute() else root / p
        return [p] if p.is_dir() else []
    packs = sorted({d.parent.parent for d in root.glob("MCPEDL/*/*/behavior_pack/recipes") if d.is_dir()})
    return [p for p in packs if include_dedicated or p.name not in DEDICATED]


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="every pack under MCPEDL/*/* (default when no --project)")
    ap.add_argument("--project", help="one pack folder, e.g. MCPEDL/Addons/Mining-Helmet")
    ap.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="resolve everything and write nothing")
    ap.add_argument("--sheet", action="store_true", help="also write assets/all_recipes_sheet.png")
    ap.add_argument("--item", action="append", default=[], metavar="ID", help="export this custom item's sprite (repeatable)")
    ap.add_argument("--items-from-recipes", action="store_true", help="also export the sprite of every custom item that has a recipe")
    ap.add_argument("--include-dedicated", action="store_true", help="also process packs with their own generator")
    ap.add_argument("--root", default=str(ROOT), help="repo root")
    ap.add_argument("--samples", help="Bedrock Samples folder (default: env BEDROCK_SAMPLES_ROOT or /tmp/bedrock-samples)")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    root = Path(a.root)
    samples = Path(a.samples) if a.samples else None
    if a.item and not a.project:
        ap.error("--item needs --project")
    packs = find_packs(root, a.project, a.include_dedicated)
    if not packs:
        print("No packs with recipes found.")
        return 0 if not a.project else 1
    bad = 0
    for pack in packs:
        if a.item:
            written, problems = export_items(pack, a.item, samples)
            for p in problems:
                print(f"FAIL {pack.name}: {p}")
            for w in written:
                print(f"item   {w.relative_to(root)}")
            bad += len(problems)
        jobs, skipped, problems = plan_pack(pack, samples)
        for p in problems:
            print(f"FAIL {pack.name}: {p}")
        bad += len(problems)
        if problems:
            print(f"{pack.name}: nothing written ({len(problems)} problem(s) above).")
            continue
        if a.check:
            print(f"ok     {pack.name}: {len(jobs)} recipe(s) resolve" + (f", {len(skipped)} skipped" if skipped else ""))
            continue
        written = write_pack(pack, jobs, a.sheet) if jobs else []
        print(f"{pack.name}: {len(jobs)} recipe image(s)" + (f", {len(skipped)} other recipe(s) skipped" if skipped else ""))
        for w in written:
            print(f"  wrote {w.relative_to(root)}")
        if a.items_from_recipes and jobs:
            sprites, sprite_problems = export_items(pack, custom_result_ids(jobs), samples)
            for p in sprite_problems:
                print(f"FAIL {pack.name}: {p}")
            bad += len(sprite_problems)
            for w in sprites:
                print(f"  wrote {w.relative_to(root)}")
    print("Done." if not bad else f"{bad} problem(s). See FAIL lines.")
    return 1 if bad else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## FILE: scripts/convert_crafting_recipe_svgs_to_png.py (45 lines, 1854 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Export SVG assets to PNG. Crafting-table PNG is a derivative of CRAFTING_TABLE.svg, never a separate reference."""
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
import base64
import re

import cairosvg

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ASSETS = ROOT / "assets"

def embed_remote_images(svg: str) -> str:
    pattern = re.compile(r'(<image\b[^>]*?\b(?:href|xlink:href)=["\'])(https?://[^"\']+)(["\'][^>]*>)', re.I)
    def replace(match):
        url = match.group(2)
        request = Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "ALNStudios-RecipeImageBuilder/1.0"})
        try:
            with urlopen(request, timeout=30) as response:
                data = response.read()
                mime = response.headers.get_content_type()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Unable to fetch linked recipe texture {url}: {exc}") from exc
        encoded = base64.b64encode(data).decode("ascii")
        return match.group(1) + f"data:{mime};base64,{encoded}" + match.group(3)
    return pattern.sub(replace, svg)

def main():
    candidates = sorted(ASSETS.glob("CRAFTING_RECIPE*.svg"))
    # Always export the crafting board template to PNG as well.
    template_svg = ROOT / "scripts" / "CRAFTING_TABLE.svg"
    if template_svg.exists():
        candidates.insert(0, template_svg)
    if not candidates:
        print("No crafting recipe SVGs found; nothing to convert.")
        return
    for source in candidates:
        output = source.with_suffix(".png")
        svg = source.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        svg = embed_remote_images(svg)
        cairosvg.svg2png(bytestring=svg.encode("utf-8"), write_to=str(output), output_width=400 if source == template_svg else 800)
        print(f"Generated {output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## FILE: scripts/ensure_recipe_unlocks.py (36 lines, 1540 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Ensure every custom shaped/shapeless crafting recipe is always unlocked."""
import json
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SEARCH_ROOTS = (ROOT / "MCPEDL" / "Addons",)
RECIPE_TYPES = {"minecraft:recipe_shaped", "minecraft:recipe_shapeless"}
changed = 0
already_correct = 0
skipped = 0

for search_root in SEARCH_ROOTS:
    if not search_root.is_dir():
        continue
    for path in sorted(search_root.glob("*/behavior_pack/recipes/**/*.json")):
        try:
            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
            raise SystemExit(f"Cannot read recipe JSON {path.relative_to(ROOT)}: {exc}")
        recipe_type = next((key for key in RECIPE_TYPES if key in data), None)
        if recipe_type is None:
            skipped += 1
            continue
        recipe = data[recipe_type]
        # Bedrock uses a single unlock object for shaped recipes and an array for shapeless recipes.
        desired = {"context": "AlwaysUnlocked"} if recipe_type == "minecraft:recipe_shaped" else [{"context": "AlwaysUnlocked"}]
        if recipe.get("unlock") == desired:
            already_correct += 1
            continue
        recipe["unlock"] = desired
        path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        changed += 1

print(f"AlwaysUnlocked recipes updated: {changed}; already correct: {already_correct}; non-shaped/shapeless recipes skipped: {skipped}")
```

## FILE: scripts/make_pack_icon.py (121 lines, 4708 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Guarantee every pack has a valid pack_icon.png (256x256 PNG, pack root).

Used two ways:
  * CLI (refresh workflow):  python3 scripts/make_pack_icon.py [--strict]
  * Imported by auto_bump_and_build.py so EVERY build gets the icon, not only
    the "Refresh Add-ons and Rebuild Packs" workflow.

Source lookup inside each behavior_pack/ and resource_pack/ (top level only,
case-insensitive): pack_icon.*, icon.*, logo.* in png/jpg/jpeg/webp/bmp/gif.
Fallbacks when a pack has none: the sibling pack's pack_icon.png, then an
icon image at the project root. Leftover source files are deleted after
conversion. Packs that still have no icon are reported loudly.
"""
import glob, os, sys
from PIL import Image, ImageOps

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
SIZE = 256
EXTS = {".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp", ".bmp", ".gif"}
STEMS = ["pack_icon", "packicon", "icon", "logo"]   # priority order
PACKS = ("behavior_pack", "resource_pack")


def _norm(stem):
    return stem.lower().replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_")


def _find_sources(folder):
    found = []
    for name in os.listdir(folder):
        stem, ext = os.path.splitext(name)
        if ext.lower() in EXTS and _norm(stem) in STEMS and name != "pack_icon.png":
            found.append(name)
    return sorted(found, key=lambda n: (STEMS.index(_norm(os.path.splitext(n)[0])), n))


def _convert(src, dest):
    img = ImageOps.exif_transpose(Image.open(src)).convert("RGBA")
    side = min(img.size)
    left, top = (img.width - side) // 2, (img.height - side) // 2
    img = img.crop((left, top, left + side, top + side))
    img = img.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
    tmp = dest + ".tmp"
    img.save(tmp, "PNG", optimize=True)
    os.replace(tmp, dest)


def _rel(p):
    return os.path.relpath(p, ROOT)


def ensure_pack_icon(pack_dir, fallbacks=(), warn=True):
    """Return True when pack_dir ends up with a usable pack_icon.png."""
    dest = os.path.join(pack_dir, "pack_icon.png")
    sources = _find_sources(pack_dir)
    try:
        if sources:
            _convert(os.path.join(pack_dir, sources[0]), dest)
            for s in sources:
                os.remove(os.path.join(pack_dir, s))
            print(f"[Pack Icon] {_rel(pack_dir)}/{sources[0]} -> pack_icon.png (sources removed: {len(sources)})")
            return True
        if os.path.isfile(dest):
            with Image.open(dest) as im:
                w, h = im.size
            if w != h or w > SIZE:
                _convert(dest, dest)
                print(f"[Pack Icon] {_rel(dest)} normalized to {SIZE}x{SIZE}")
            return True
        for fb in fallbacks:
            if os.path.isfile(fb):
                _convert(fb, dest)
                print(f"[Pack Icon] {_rel(dest)} created from {_rel(fb)}")
                return True
    except Exception as exc:  # corrupt image etc.
        print(f"[Pack Icon] ERROR in {_rel(pack_dir)}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return False
    if warn:
        print(f"[Pack Icon] WARNING: {_rel(pack_dir)} has NO pack_icon.png "
              f"(add icon.png next to manifest.json)", file=sys.stderr)
    return False


def ensure_project_icons(project_path):
    """Handle both packs of one project. Returns list of pack dirs still missing an icon."""
    dirs = {p: os.path.join(project_path, p) for p in PACKS}
    present = [p for p in PACKS if os.path.isdir(dirs[p])]
    project_srcs = []
    if os.path.isdir(project_path):
        project_srcs = [os.path.join(project_path, n) for n in _find_sources(project_path)]
        pi = os.path.join(project_path, "pack_icon.png")
        if os.path.isfile(pi):
            project_srcs.insert(0, pi)
    missing = []
    # first pass: packs that have their own icon; second pass: fill gaps from siblings
    done = {p: ensure_pack_icon(dirs[p], fallbacks=project_srcs, warn=False) for p in present}
    for p in present:
        if not done[p]:
            sib = [os.path.join(dirs[q], "pack_icon.png") for q in present if q != p]
            if not ensure_pack_icon(dirs[p], fallbacks=sib):
                missing.append(dirs[p])
    return missing


def main():
    strict = "--strict" in sys.argv
    missing, n = [], 0
    for project in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, "MCPEDL", "*", "*"))):
        if not os.path.isdir(project):
            continue
        if any(os.path.isdir(os.path.join(project, p)) for p in PACKS):
            n += 1
            missing += ensure_project_icons(project)
    print(f"[Pack Icon] checked {n} project(s); {len(missing)} pack(s) without icon.")
    return 1 if (strict and missing) else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## FILE: scripts/optimize_images.py (54 lines, 1831 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse, io, sys
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
MAX_WIDTH = 800
MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024


def optimize(path, check):
    original = path.read_bytes()
    img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(original))
    needs = img.width > MAX_WIDTH or len(original) > MAX_BYTES
    if check:
        return needs, len(original)
    if not needs:
        return False, len(original)
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    if img.width > MAX_WIDTH:
        img = img.resize((MAX_WIDTH, round(img.height * MAX_WIDTH / img.width)), Image.LANCZOS)
    best = None
    for quality in range(85, 34, -5):
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        img.save(buf, "JPEG", quality=quality, optimize=True, progressive=True)
        best = buf.getvalue()
        if len(best) <= MAX_BYTES:
            break
    path.write_bytes(best)
    return True, len(best)


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Auto resize (max 800 px wide) and compress (max 200 KB) JPEG files.")
    p.add_argument("folder", nargs="?", default=str(ROOT / "assets" / "Images"))
    p.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="Report only; exit 1 if any image is too wide or too large.")
    args = p.parse_args()
    folder = Path(args.folder)
    files = sorted(f for f in folder.glob("*") if f.suffix.lower() in (".jpg", ".jpeg"))
    if not files:
        print(f"No JPEG files in {folder}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    bad = 0
    for f in files:
        changed, size = optimize(f, args.check)
        bad += changed
        state = "needs optimizing" if args.check and changed else "optimized" if changed else "ok"
        print(f"{state:16} {f.name} {size // 1024} KB")
    return 1 if args.check and bad else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## FILE: scripts/manifest_tools.py (436 lines, 19031 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Manifest helpers shared by the build, publish and maintenance scripts.

What lives here
  * align_project_manifests - make every source manifest of a project share ONE version
    (the highest, or an explicit target): header.version, the "(vX.Y.Z)" title suffix,
    module versions and BP->RP dependency versions. Pack UUIDs are never touched.
  * bump_patch - the next X.Y.(Z+1) after the highest manifest version.

Versions are exactly X.Y.Z everywhere: header.version, metadata.version, the "(vX.Y.Z)" title suffix, changelog headings, package names and build snapshots.
  * ensure_metadata - fill metadata.authors (and metadata.license when MANIFEST_LICENSE is set).
  * compute_source_hash - a content hash of a project's source that ignores everything the
    build itself rewrites (manifest versions/titles, derived world copies, docs, archives),
    so "did the source change since the last snapshot?" has a stable answer.

CLI (explicit maintenance)
  python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --project MCPEDL/Addons/Mining-Helmet --check
  python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --project MCPEDL/Addons/Mining-Helmet --align
  python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --project MCPEDL/Addons/Mining-Helmet --bump
  python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --project MCPEDL/Addons/Mining-Helmet --set 1.0.3
  python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --all --check
"""
import argparse
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import sys

REPO_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
INSTALLED_COPY_PARTS = {"world", "worlds", "behavior_packs", "resource_packs"}
JUNK_DIRS = {"__pycache__", ".git", ".github", "node_modules"}
JUNK_FILES = {".ds_store", "thumbs.db", "desktop.ini", ".gitkeep"}
BINARY_EXT = {".mcpack", ".mcaddon", ".mcworld", ".zip"}
TITLE_SUFFIX_RE = re.compile(r"(?:\s*\(v(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)*)\))+\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)
HAS_SUFFIX_RE = re.compile(r"\(v(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)*)\)\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)
FULL_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:v)?(\d+)(?:\.(\d+)\.(\d+))?$", re.IGNORECASE)
AUTHOR = "ALNStudios"
LEGACY_AUTHORS = ("ALNStudios",)
FILENAME_TEMPLATE_ENV = "PACK_FILENAME_TEMPLATE"
DEFAULT_FILENAME_TEMPLATE = "{shortcut} V{version}"
SHORTCUTS_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "pack_shortcuts.json")
_SHORTCUT_SKIP_WORDS = {"and", "of", "the", "in", "a", "to", "for", "payload"}
_PACK_WORD_RE = re.compile(r"\s+(?:BP|RP|Behavior Pack|Resource Pack|Behaviour Pack)\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)
# The only derived copy a map build writes inside its own source tree (see the builder).
DERIVED_WORLD_COPY = "world/behavior_packs/OneBlockVoid/"


def is_triple(value):
    return (isinstance(value, list) and len(value) == 3
            and all(type(p) is int and p >= 0 for p in value))


def fmt(version):
    return ".".join(str(p) for p in version)


def source_manifest_paths(project_path):
    """Source manifests only; installed world copies and node_modules are ignored."""
    found = []
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(project_path):
        dirs[:] = sorted(d for d in dirs if d not in JUNK_DIRS)
        if "manifest.json" not in files:
            continue
        rel = os.path.relpath(root, project_path).replace(os.sep, "/")
        parts = set() if rel == "." else set(rel.split("/"))
        if parts & INSTALLED_COPY_PARTS:
            continue
        found.append(os.path.join(root, "manifest.json"))
    return sorted(found)


def _read(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        return json.load(fh)


def _dump(data):
    return json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n"


def highest_version(project_path):
    versions = []
    for path in source_manifest_paths(project_path):
        try:
            v = _read(path).get("header", {}).get("version")
        except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
            continue
        if is_triple(v):
            versions.append(v)
    return max(versions) if versions else None


def parse_full(text):
    """Parse supported project version format: vX or X.Y.Z."""
    cleaned = str(text or "").strip()
    m = FULL_RE.fullmatch(cleaned)
    if not m:
        return None
    major = int(m.group(1))
    # If 3 parts were given
    if m.group(2) is not None and m.group(3) is not None:
        return [major, int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3))]
    return [major, 0, 0]

def highest_full(project_path):
    return highest_version(project_path)

def bump_patch(version):
    if not is_triple(version):
        raise ValueError("Version must contain exactly three non-negative integers")
    if version[1] == 0 and version[2] == 0:
        return [version[0] + 1, 0, 0]
    return [version[0], version[1], version[2] + 1]

def _retitle(name, version_text):
    """Replace an existing terminal (vX.Y.Z) suffix. Titles without one are left alone:
    the title validator reports them, it never silently rewrites them."""
    if isinstance(name, str) and HAS_SUFFIX_RE.search(name):
        return f"{TITLE_SUFFIX_RE.sub('', name).rstrip()} (v{version_text})"
    return name


def _migrate_legacy_name(value):
    if isinstance(value, str):
        for old in LEGACY_AUTHORS:
            value = re.sub(r"(?<![/\w])" + re.escape(old), AUTHOR, value)
    return value


def migrate_legacy_branding(data):
    """Rename the old creator name in header.description and metadata.authors. True if changed."""
    changed = False
    header = data.get("header")
    if isinstance(header, dict) and isinstance(header.get("description"), str):
        new = _migrate_legacy_name(header["description"])
        if new != header["description"]:
            header["description"] = new
            changed = True
    meta = data.get("metadata")
    if isinstance(meta, dict) and isinstance(meta.get("authors"), list):
        new = [_migrate_legacy_name(a) for a in meta["authors"]]
        if new != meta["authors"]:
            meta["authors"] = new
            changed = True
    return changed


def ensure_metadata(data, license_text=None):
    """Fill metadata.authors (and metadata.license if given). Returns True if changed."""
    changed = migrate_legacy_branding(data)
    meta = data.get("metadata")
    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
        meta = {}
        data["metadata"] = meta
        changed = True
    authors = meta.get("authors")
    if not (isinstance(authors, list) and any(isinstance(a, str) and a.strip() for a in authors)):
        meta["authors"] = [AUTHOR]
        changed = True
    if license_text and not meta.get("license"):
        meta["license"] = license_text
        changed = True
    return changed


def align_project_manifests(project_path, target=None, write=True, metadata=True):
    """Align every source manifest to one Minecraft-compatible X.Y.Z version."""
    paths = source_manifest_paths(project_path)
    loaded = []
    for path in paths:
        try: data = _read(path)
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            print(f"WARNING: unreadable manifest skipped: {path}", file=sys.stderr); continue
        if isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("header"), dict): loaded.append((path, data))
    if not loaded: return None, []
    if target is None:
        versions = [d["header"]["version"] for _, d in loaded if is_triple(d["header"].get("version"))]
        if not versions: return None, []
        target = max(versions)
    target = list(target)
    if not is_triple(target): raise ValueError("Target version must be exactly X.Y.Z")
    target_text = fmt(target)
    own_uuids = {d["header"].get("uuid") for _, d in loaded}
    license_text = os.environ.get("MANIFEST_LICENSE", "").strip() or None
    changed = []
    for path, data in loaded:
        before = copy.deepcopy(data)
        header = data["header"]
        meta = data.get("metadata")
        if not isinstance(meta, dict): meta = {}; data["metadata"] = meta
        meta["version"] = target_text
        header["version"] = target
        for module in data.get("modules", []) or []:
            if isinstance(module, dict) and isinstance(module.get("version"), list): module["version"] = target
        title_text = str(target[0]) if (target[1] == 0 and target[2] == 0) else target_text
        retitled = _retitle(header.get("name"), title_text)
        if retitled != header.get("name"): header["name"] = retitled
        deps = data.get("dependencies", [])
        for dep in deps if isinstance(deps, list) else []:
            if isinstance(dep, dict) and dep.get("uuid") in own_uuids and isinstance(dep.get("version"), list): dep["version"] = target
        migrate_legacy_branding(data)
        if metadata and os.environ.get("MANIFEST_METADATA", "1") != "0": ensure_metadata(data, license_text)
        if data != before:
            if write:
                with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh: fh.write(_dump(data))
            changed.append(os.path.relpath(path, REPO_ROOT).replace(os.sep, "/"))
    return target, changed

def _normalized_manifest_bytes(raw, own_uuids):
    """Manifest content minus everything the version machinery rewrites."""
    try:
        data = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
        header = data.get("header", {})
        header.pop("version", None)
        if isinstance(data.get("metadata"), dict):
            data["metadata"].pop("version", None)
        if isinstance(header.get("name"), str):
            header["name"] = TITLE_SUFFIX_RE.sub("", header["name"]).rstrip()
        for module in data.get("modules", []) or []:
            if isinstance(module, dict):
                module.pop("version", None)
        for dep in data.get("dependencies", []) or []:
            if isinstance(dep, dict) and dep.get("uuid") in own_uuids:
                dep.pop("version", None)
        return json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
    except (ValueError, AttributeError, UnicodeDecodeError):
        return raw


def is_source_rel_path(rel):
    """Which project files count as pack source for change detection."""
    low = rel.lower()
    base = os.path.basename(low)
    if base in JUNK_FILES or base.endswith(("~", ".bak", ".tmp", ".orig", ".importing")):
        return False
    if base.endswith(".md") or base in {"license", "license.txt", "notice", "notice.txt", "lock"}:
        return False
    if os.path.splitext(base)[1] in BINARY_EXT:
        return False
    if rel.startswith(DERIVED_WORLD_COPY):
        return False
    return True


def compute_source_hash(project_path):
    """Stable SHA-256 over the project's source (see is_source_rel_path)."""
    own_uuids = set()
    for path in source_manifest_paths(project_path):
        try:
            own_uuids.add(_read(path).get("header", {}).get("uuid"))
        except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
            pass
    manifest_set = set(source_manifest_paths(project_path))
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    entries = []
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(project_path):
        dirs[:] = sorted(d for d in dirs if d not in JUNK_DIRS)
        for name in sorted(files):
            full = os.path.join(root, name)
            rel = os.path.relpath(full, project_path).replace(os.sep, "/")
            if is_source_rel_path(rel):
                entries.append((rel, full))
    for rel, full in sorted(entries):
        with open(full, "rb") as fh:
            raw = fh.read()
        if full in manifest_set:
            raw = _normalized_manifest_bytes(raw, own_uuids)
        digest.update(rel.encode("utf-8") + b"\0")
        digest.update(hashlib.sha256(raw).digest())
    return digest.hexdigest()


def manifest_title(project_path):
    """Pack title taken from the source manifests: header.name without the (vX.Y.Z) suffix and
    without a trailing BP/RP word. Returns None unless every manifest agrees on one title."""
    titles = set()
    for path in source_manifest_paths(project_path):
        try:
            name = _read(path).get("header", {}).get("name")
        except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
            continue
        if not isinstance(name, str):
            continue
        name = TITLE_SUFFIX_RE.sub("", name).strip()
        name = _PACK_WORD_RE.sub("", name).strip()
        name = re.sub(r'[\\/*?:"<>|]', "", name).strip()
        if name:
            titles.add(name)
    return titles.pop() if len(titles) == 1 else None


def shortcut_from_slug(slug):
    """Short alphanumeric name built from a project folder name: no hyphens, underscores or spaces."""
    words = [w for w in re.split(r"[-_\s]+", slug) if w and w.lower() not in _SHORTCUT_SKIP_WORDS]
    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]", "", "".join(w[:1].upper() + w[1:] for w in words)) or "Pack"


def shortcut_for_slug(slug):
    """Filename shortcut of a project: scripts/pack_shortcuts.json first, else built from the folder name."""
    try:
        with open(SHORTCUTS_FILE, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            value = json.load(fh).get(slug)
    except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
        value = None
    if isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9]+", value):
        return value
    return shortcut_from_slug(slug)


def package_shortcut(project_path):
    return shortcut_for_slug(os.path.basename(os.path.normpath(project_path)))


def _manifest_filename_template(project_path):
    for path in source_manifest_paths(project_path):
        try:
            value = (_read(path).get("metadata") or {}).get("package_filename")
        except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
            continue
        if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
            return value.strip()
    return None


def package_basename(project_path, version_text, fallback_title):
    """File name (without extension) of the downloadable package.

    Title comes from the manifest header.name (fallback: the DESCRIPTION title) and the version
    from the manifest. Default: '<Shortcut> VX' (or '<Shortcut> VX.Y.Z'; see package_shortcut).
    Template precedence: manifest metadata.package_filename, then the PACK_FILENAME_TEMPLATE
    variable, then '{shortcut} V{version}'. Placeholders: {shortcut}, {title}, {version}, {author}.
    A template must contain 'V{version}' and no parenthesis because the README link repair finds
    packages by it; otherwise (including the old '(V{version})' templates) the default template is used.
    """
    title = manifest_title(project_path) or fallback_title
    shortcut = package_shortcut(project_path)
    template = (_manifest_filename_template(project_path)
                or os.environ.get(FILENAME_TEMPLATE_ENV, "").strip()
                or DEFAULT_FILENAME_TEMPLATE)
    if "V{version}" not in template or "(" in template:
        print(f"WARNING: filename template {template!r} needs 'V{{version}}' without parentheses; using the default.", file=sys.stderr)
        template = DEFAULT_FILENAME_TEMPLATE
    v_parts = str(version_text).split(".")
    display_ver = v_parts[0] if (len(v_parts) == 3 and v_parts[1] == "0" and v_parts[2] == "0") else str(version_text)
    try:
        name = template.format(shortcut=shortcut, title=title, version=display_ver, author=AUTHOR)
    except (KeyError, IndexError, ValueError):
        print(f"WARNING: bad filename template {template!r}; using the default.", file=sys.stderr)
        name = DEFAULT_FILENAME_TEMPLATE.format(shortcut=shortcut, title=title, version=display_ver, author=AUTHOR)
    return re.sub(r'[\\/*?:"<>|]', "", name).strip()


def latest_changelog_version(project_path):
    """First '## [vX]' or '## [X.Y.Z]' heading of CHANGELOG.md, or None."""
    path = os.path.join(project_path, "CHANGELOG.md")
    try:
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            for line in fh:
                m = re.match(r"^## \[(?:v)?(\d+(?:\.\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?)?)\]", line, re.IGNORECASE)
                if m:
                    ver_raw = m.group(1)
                    if "." in ver_raw:
                        parts = parse_full(ver_raw)
                        if len(ver_raw.split(".")) == 4:
                            raw = [int(p) for p in ver_raw.split(".")]
                            parts = [raw[0], raw[2], raw[3]]
                        return fmt(parts) if parts else None
                    else:
                        num = int(ver_raw)
                        return f"{num}.0.0"
    except OSError:
        pass
    return None


def _all_projects():
    out = []
    for cat in ("Addons", "Maps", "Script", "Minigame"):
        base = os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, "MCPEDL", cat)
        if os.path.isdir(base):
            out += [os.path.join(base, n) for n in sorted(os.listdir(base))
                    if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(base, n))]
    return out


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--project", action="append", help="Project path (repeatable).")
    ap.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="Every project under MCPEDL/.")
    mode = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    mode.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="Report what --align would change; write nothing.")
    mode.add_argument("--align", action="store_true", help="Align every manifest to the highest version.")
    mode.add_argument("--bump", action="store_true", help="Align to highest X.Y.Z+1.")
    mode.add_argument("--set", metavar="X.Y.Z", help="Align every manifest to this exact version (update scripts use it). Never lowers a version.")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if args.all:
        projects = _all_projects()
    elif args.project:
        projects = [p if os.path.isabs(p) else os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, p) for p in args.project]
    else:
        ap.error("pass --project or --all")

    dirty = False
    for project in projects:
        high = highest_version(project)
        if high is None:
            print(f"{os.path.relpath(project, REPO_ROOT)}: no source manifest")
            continue
        target = bump_patch(high) if args.bump else None
        if args.set:
            target = parse_full(args.set)
            if target is None:
                print(f"--set needs exactly X.Y.Z, got: {args.set}", file=sys.stderr)
                return 2
            if target < high:
                print(f"{os.path.relpath(project, REPO_ROOT)}: refusing to lower v{fmt(high)} to v{fmt(target)}", file=sys.stderr)
                return 2
        _, changed = align_project_manifests(project, target=target, write=not args.check)
        label = os.path.relpath(project, REPO_ROOT)
        if changed:
            dirty = True
            verb = "would change" if args.check else "changed"
            print(f"{label}: v{fmt(high)} -> v{fmt(target or high)}; {verb} {len(changed)} manifest(s)")
            for c in changed:
                print(f"    {c}")
        else:
            print(f"{label}: v{fmt(high)} consistent")
    return 1 if (args.check and dirty) else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

## FILE: scripts/doc_template.py (539 lines, 29795 bytes)
