#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Strict README, DESCRIPTION, and CHANGELOG template check (Part 2). Used by build_payload.py and as an audit tool.

  python3 scripts/doc_template.py --all           audit every project under MCPEDL/ (report only)
  python3 scripts/doc_template.py --project NAME  audit one project
  python3 scripts/doc_template.py PATH            audit a folder (a payload folder or a project folder)
"""
import argparse, datetime, json, os, re, sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CF_URL = "https://www.curseforge.com/members/allenscreations/projects"
MP_URL = "https://mcpedl.com/user/alnstudios/"
# The repo is private, so raw.githubusercontent.com URLs no longer load on CurseForge/MCPEDL.
# Set LISTING_IMAGE_BASE (repo variable or env) to a PUBLIC folder holding curseforge.jpg and MCPEDL.jpg.
IMG_BASE = os.environ.get("LISTING_IMAGE_BASE", "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/Images/")
if not IMG_BASE.endswith("/"):
    IMG_BASE += "/"
IMG_MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024
LISTINGS = {
    "CurseForge": ("https://www.curseforge.com/", "curseforge.jpg", CF_URL),
    "MCPEDL": ("https://mcpedl.com/", "MCPEDL.jpg", MP_URL),
}


def listing_html(label, href=None):
    prefix, file, default = LISTINGS[label]
    return f'<a href="{href or default}"><img src="{IMG_BASE}{file}" alt="{label}" width="45%"></a>'


LISTING_LINE = listing_html("CurseForge") + " " + listing_html("MCPEDL")
DESCRIPTION_BADGES = (
    "[![Minecraft Bedrock](https://img.shields.io/badge/Minecraft%20Bedrock-Stable%20Release-brightgreen?style=flat-square&logo=minecraft)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges)",
    "[![Format](https://img.shields.io/badge/Format-.MCPACK%20%7C%20.MCADDON%20%7C%20.MCWORLD-blue?style=flat-square)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges)",
)
LEGACY_BADGES = (
    "[![Minecraft Bedrock](https://img.shields.io/badge/Minecraft%20Bedrock-1.21%20--%201.26%2B-brightgreen?style=flat-square&logo=minecraft)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges)",
)
GUIDELINES = """## Guidelines and Attribution

- Creator: ALNStudios
- Rights & Reservation: All rights reserved for ALNStudios.
- Content Creators: Content creators are permitted to feature this creation in videos and streams with credit to ALNStudios.
- Redistribution: Do not re-upload this pack or provide direct monetized download bypasses. Link directly to the official repository page.
- Disclaimer: This is an unofficial community creation. It is not an official Minecraft product and is not approved by or associated with Mojang or Microsoft.

---

© All rights reserved for ALNStudios.

---

"""
DESCRIPTION_SECTIONS = ("Overview", "Key Features", "How to Use", "Compatibility", "Creator and Permissions",
                        "Guidelines and Attribution", "Search & Discovery Keywords")
COMPAT_LABELS = ("Minecraft Bedrock Edition", "Experimental features", "Achievements", "Verification")
ACHIEVEMENT = ("likely achievement-friendly", "not achievement-friendly", "unknown / needs confirmation")
VERIFIED = ("source created", "package built", "in-game tested")
KEYWORD_ANCHORS = ("Minecraft Bedrock add-on", "MCPEDL add-on", "CurseForge Bedrock", "ALNStudios")
CHANGELOG_GROUPS = ("Added", "Improved & Balanced", "Fixed")
INSTALL_STEPS = ("1. Download the package using the link above.",
                 "2. Open the downloaded file with Minecraft to import it.",
                 "3. If the package contains a pack that must be enabled separately, activate it in your world's settings.")
INFO_LINE = re.compile(r"^For gameplay features, usage details, compatibility notes, and other project information, read the \[full description\]\(([^)\s]+)\)\.$")
EMOJI = re.compile("[\U0001F300-\U0001FAFF\u2600-\u27BF\uFE0F]")
BANNED = (
    ("a Markdown table", re.compile(r"(?m)^\s*\|.*\|\s*$")),
    ("a code block", re.compile(r"```")),
    ("a badge", re.compile(r"img\.shields\.io|badge", re.I)),
    ("'BP' or 'RP' (write Behavior Pack / Resource Pack)", re.compile(r"\b(?:BP|RP)\b")),
    ("'Script API' or an @minecraft module name", re.compile(r"Script API|@minecraft/", re.I)),
    ("a raw identifier such as minecraft:diamond", re.compile(r"\bminecraft:[a-z0-9_]+")),
    ("an emoji", EMOJI),
    ("a duplicate version suffix such as (v1.0.0) (v1.0.1)", re.compile(r"\(v\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?\)\s*\(v\d+")),
)


def norm(text):
    return "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n"))


def manifest_version(root):
    try:
        data = json.loads((Path(root) / "behavior_pack/manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        v = data["header"]["version"]
        if isinstance(v, list) and len(v) == 3 and all(type(x) is int for x in v):
            return ".".join(map(str, v))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def vtuple(text):
    return tuple(int(x) for x in text.split("."))


def banned_content(name, text, errors, images_allowed=False):
    if images_allowed:
        for badge in DESCRIPTION_BADGES + LEGACY_BADGES:
            text = text.replace(badge, "")
    for label, rx in BANNED:
        if label == "a badge" and images_allowed:
            rx = re.compile(r"img\.shields\.io", re.I)
        if rx.search(text):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {name} must not contain {label} (Part 2).")
    if not images_allowed and (re.search(r"!\[[^\]]*\]\(", text) or re.search(r"<img\b", text, re.I)):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {name} must not contain images (Part 2).")


def check_description_badges(text, errors):
    lines = text.splitlines()
    for badge in DESCRIPTION_BADGES:
        if lines.count(badge) != 1:
            errors.append(f"DOC: DESCRIPTION.md must include this badge exactly once: {badge}")
    for badge in LEGACY_BADGES:
        if badge in lines:
            errors.append("DOC: DESCRIPTION.md uses the old hardcoded-version Minecraft Bedrock badge; run apply_description_policy.")


def check_listing_images(creators, errors, images_dir=None):
    n = "DESCRIPTION.md"
    for label, (prefix, file, default) in LISTINGS.items():
        rx = re.compile(r'<a href="(https://[^"\s]+)"><img src="' + re.escape(IMG_BASE + file) + r'" alt="' + label + r'" width="45%"></a>')
        found = rx.findall(creators)
        if len(found) != 1:
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} '## Creator and Permissions' must contain the clickable {label} image exactly once, e.g. {listing_html(label)}")
        elif not found[0].startswith(prefix):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the {label} image link must point to {prefix}")
    if len(re.findall(r"<img\b", creators, re.I)) != 2 or re.search(r"\bheight=", creators, re.I):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the Creator section may hold only the two listing images, with relative width and no fixed height.")
    folder = Path(images_dir) if images_dir else ROOT / "assets" / "Images"
    for label, (prefix, file, default) in LISTINGS.items():
        f = folder / file
        if f.is_file() and f.stat().st_size > IMG_MAX_BYTES:
            errors.append(f"DOC: {file} is {f.stat().st_size // 1024} KB; run scripts/optimize_images.py (limit {IMG_MAX_BYTES // 1024} KB).")


def apply_description_policy(text, addon=False):
    drop = DESCRIPTION_BADGES + LEGACY_BADGES
    lines = [line for line in norm(text).split("\n") if line not in drop]
    insert_at = next((i + 1 for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.strip() == "**By ALNStudios**"), None)
    if insert_at is None:
        insert_at = next((i + 1 for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.startswith("# ")), 0)
    while insert_at < len(lines) and not lines[insert_at].strip():
        lines.pop(insert_at)
    lines[insert_at:insert_at] = ["", *DESCRIPTION_BADGES, ""]
    if addon:
        found = False
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            if re.match(r"^- (?:\*\*)?Achievements(?:\*\*)?:", line):
                lines[i] = "- Achievements: Likely achievement-friendly."
                found = True
        if not found:
            compatibility = next((i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.strip().lower() == "## compatibility"), None)
            if compatibility is not None:
                insert_at = next((i for i in range(compatibility + 1, len(lines)) if lines[i].startswith("## ")), len(lines))
                lines.insert(insert_at, "- Achievements: Likely achievement-friendly.")
    return apply_listing_images("\n".join(lines))


def apply_listing_images(text):
    text = norm(text)
    if "<img" in text and LISTING_LINE in text:
        return text
    lines = text.split("\n")
    start = next((i for i, l in enumerate(lines) if l.strip() == "## Creator and Permissions"), None)
    if start is None:
        return text
    end = next((i for i in range(start + 1, len(lines)) if lines[i].startswith("## ")), len(lines))
    while end > start + 1 and not lines[end - 1].strip():
        end -= 1
    lines[end:end] = ["", LISTING_LINE]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def check_project_readme(text, errors):
    lines = [l for l in norm(text).split("\n") if l.strip()]
    n = "README.md"
    if len(lines) != 11:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} must have exactly 11 non-empty lines in the version-free download template (found {len(lines)}).")
    title = re.match(r"^# (.+)$", lines[0]) if lines else None
    if not title:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} line 1 must be '# [Pack Name]' with no release version.")
        return
    name = title.group(1)
    if re.search(r"\(v\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?\)$", name, re.I):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} title must not contain a release version.")
    expect = [
        (1, f"Download the current release of **{name}** for Minecraft Bedrock Edition.", "the intro sentence"),
        (2, "## Download", "the '## Download' heading"),
        (5, "## Installation", "the '## Installation' heading"),
        (6, INSTALL_STEPS[0], "installation step 1"),
        (7, INSTALL_STEPS[1], "installation step 2"),
        (9, "## Pack Information", "the '## Pack Information' heading"),
    ]
    for index, wanted, label in expect:
        if index >= len(lines) or lines[index] != wanted:
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} {label} must read exactly: {wanted}")
    fmt = re.match(r"^- \*\*Package format:\*\* `\.(mcaddon|mcpack|mcworld)`$", lines[3]) if len(lines) > 3 else None
    if not fmt:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} line 4 must name a supported package format: `.mcaddon`, `.mcpack`, or `.mcworld`.")
    dl = re.match(r"^- \*\*Direct download:\*\* \[Download (.+) \(\.(mcaddon|mcpack|mcworld)\)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)", lines[4]) if len(lines) > 4 else None
    if not dl:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} line 5 must be a direct download link for {name} and its package format.")
    else:
        if dl.group(1) != name and not dl.group(1).startswith(name + " — "):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the download link text must name '{name}'.")
        if fmt and dl.group(2) != fmt.group(1):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the package format and the download link extension must match.")
    if fmt and fmt.group(1) == "mcworld" and len(lines) > 8:
        expected_install = "3. Find the imported world in your Worlds list and open it."
    else:
        expected_install = INSTALL_STEPS[2]
    if len(lines) > 8 and lines[8] != expected_install:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} installation step 3 must read exactly: {expected_install}")
    info = INFO_LINE.match(lines[10]) if len(lines) > 10 else None
    if not info:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the last line must link the full DESCRIPTION.md.")
    elif "DESCRIPTION.md" not in info.group(1):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the full description link must point to DESCRIPTION.md.")
    banned_content(n, text, errors)


def section_map(text):
    parts = re.split(r"(?m)^(?=## )", text)
    return parts[0], [(p.split("\n", 1)[0][3:].strip(), p.split("\n", 1)[1] if "\n" in p else "") for p in parts[1:]]


def check_description(text, version, errors, ptype="addon", images_dir=None):
    n = "DESCRIPTION.md"
    text = norm(text)
    pre, sections = section_map(text)
    head = re.search(r"(?m)^# (.+?) — (\S.*)$", pre)
    if not head or pre.lstrip().split("\n", 1)[0] != head.group(0):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} line 1 must be '# [Pack Name] — [Clear, factual subtitle]' with no release version.")
    elif re.search(r"\(v\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?\)$", head.group(1), re.I):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} title must not contain a release version.")
    after = [l for l in pre.split("\n")[1:] if l.strip()]
    if not after or after[0] != "**By ALNStudios**":
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the line after the title must be exactly: **By ALNStudios**")
    elif len(after) < 2 or after[1].startswith(("#", "-", "|", "*")):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} needs a short summary paragraph after the By line and before ## Overview.")
    names = [s[0] for s in sections]
    for opt in ("Commands", "Common Questions"):
        at = names.index("How to Use") + 1 if "How to Use" in names else None
        if at is None or at >= len(names) or names[at] != opt:
            continue
        opt_body = sections[at][1]
        sections = sections[:at] + sections[at + 1:]
        names = [s[0] for s in sections]
        opt_lines = [l for l in opt_body.split("\n") if l.strip()]
        if not opt_lines:
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} '## {opt}' must not be empty; remove it or add verified lines.")
        pat = r"^- \*\*[^*]+\*\*: \S" if opt == "Common Questions" else r"^- `?/\S.*`?: \S"
        for l in opt_lines:
            if not re.match(pat, l):
                want = "- **Question**: answer" if opt == "Common Questions" else "- `/command options`: what it does"
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} '## {opt}' lines must read '{want}'.")
                break
    if names != list(DESCRIPTION_SECTIONS):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the ## sections must be exactly, in this order: {', '.join(DESCRIPTION_SECTIONS)}. Found: {', '.join(names) or 'none'}.")
        return banned_content(n, text, errors, images_allowed=True)
    body = dict(sections)
    key = body["Key Features"].strip("\n")
    cards = re.split(r"(?m)^(?=### )", key)
    if not key.strip() or not key.lstrip().startswith("### "):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} '## Key Features' must contain only '### Feature Name' cards.")
    else:
        for card in cards:
            if not card.strip():
                continue
            if not re.search(r"(?m)^- \*\*What Happens\*\*: \S", card):
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the feature card '{card.splitlines()[0][4:].strip()}' needs a '- **What Happens**: ...' bullet.")
    for heading in ("Overview", "How to Use"):
        if not body[heading].strip():
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} '## {heading}' must not be empty.")
    compat = [l for l in body["Compatibility"].split("\n") if l.strip()]
    found = []
    for line in compat:
        m = re.match(r"^- (Minecraft Bedrock Edition|Experimental features|Achievements|Verification): (\S.*)$", line)
        found.append((m.group(1), m.group(2)) if m else None)
    if [f[0] if f else None for f in found] != list(COMPAT_LABELS):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} '## Compatibility' must be exactly four bullets, in this order: " + ", ".join(f"'- {l}: ...'" for l in COMPAT_LABELS) + ".")
    else:
        ach, ver = found[2][1].lower(), found[3][1].lower()
        if not any(a in ach for a in ACHIEVEMENT):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the Achievements line must use one of: Likely achievement-friendly, Not achievement-friendly, Unknown / needs confirmation.")
        if ptype == "addon" and not ach.startswith("likely achievement-friendly"):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} add-on Achievements must start with 'Likely achievement-friendly'.")
        if not any(ver.startswith(v) for v in VERIFIED):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the Verification line must start with 'Source created', 'Package built', or 'In-game tested'.")
    creators = body["Creator and Permissions"]
    for label, url in (("CurseForge", CF_URL), ("MCPEDL", MP_URL)):
        if url not in creators:
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} '## Creator and Permissions' must link the {label} profile ({url}).")
    check_listing_images(creators, errors, images_dir)
    if GUIDELINES + "## Search & Discovery Keywords" not in text:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the attribution block must be the exact Part 2 text, directly before '## Search & Discovery Keywords'.")
    kw = body["Search & Discovery Keywords"]
    for anchor in KEYWORD_ANCHORS:
        if anchor.lower() not in kw.lower():
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the keyword section must include '{anchor}'.")
    banned_content(n, text, errors, images_allowed=True)


def check_changelog(text, version, errors):
    n = "CHANGELOG.md"
    text = norm(text)
    lines = text.split("\n")
    ne = [(i, l) for i, l in enumerate(lines) if l.strip()]
    if not ne or not re.match(r"^# Changelog — \S.*$", ne[0][1]):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} line 1 must be '# Changelog — [Add-on Name]'.")
        return
    if len(ne) < 2 or ne[1][1].strip() != "---":
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the title must be followed directly by a '---' line, with no boilerplate.")
    versions, group_order, current, bullets_in_group = [], -1, None, 0
    in_entries = False
    for i, line in ne[2:]:
        entry = re.match(r"^## \[(\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\] - (\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})(?: - \S.*)?$", line)
        group = re.match(r"^### (.+)$", line)
        bullet = re.match(r"^- \*\*[^*]+\*\*: \S", line)
        if entry:
            if current and bullets_in_group == 0:
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} entry {current} has an empty group.")
            try:
                datetime.date.fromisoformat(entry.group(2))
            except ValueError:
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} entry {entry.group(1)} has an invalid date {entry.group(2)}.")
            versions.append(entry.group(1))
            current, group_order, bullets_in_group, in_entries = entry.group(1), -1, 0, True
        elif group:
            if not in_entries:
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} a '### {group.group(1)}' group appears before any release entry.")
                continue
            if group.group(1) not in CHANGELOG_GROUPS:
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} group '### {group.group(1)}' is not allowed; use Added, Improved & Balanced, or Fixed.")
                continue
            idx = CHANGELOG_GROUPS.index(group.group(1))
            if idx <= group_order:
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} entry {current}: groups must appear once, in the order Added, Improved & Balanced, Fixed.")
            if group_order >= 0 and bullets_in_group == 0:
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} entry {current} has an empty group.")
            group_order, bullets_in_group = idx, 0
        elif bullet:
            if group_order < 0:
                errors.append(f"DOC: {n} entry {current}: bullets must be under a ### group.")
            bullets_in_group += 1
        else:
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} line {i + 1} is not allowed: only release headings, ### groups, and '- **Title**: text' bullets.")
    if current and bullets_in_group == 0:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} entry {current} has an empty group.")
    if not versions:
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} needs at least one entry like '## [1.0.0] - YYYY-MM-DD'.")
    else:
        if [vtuple(v) for v in versions] != sorted((vtuple(v) for v in versions), reverse=True) or len(set(versions)) != len(versions):
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} entries must be newest first with no duplicate versions.")
        if version and versions[0] != version:
            errors.append(f"DOC: {n} the newest entry is {versions[0]} but the manifest version is {version}.")
    if re.search(r"https?://|\]\(|www\.", text):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} must not contain links or URLs (Part 2).")
    if re.search(r"keep a changelog|semantic versioning", text, re.I):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} must not mention Keep a Changelog or Semantic Versioning.")
    if EMOJI.search(text):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} must not contain emojis.")
    if re.search(r"\.(?:js|json|mcfunction)\b|\bminecraft:[a-z_]+", text):
        errors.append(f"DOC: {n} must not name files, functions, or identifiers; describe player-visible changes.")


def check_docs(root, ptype, images_dir=None):
    root = Path(root)
    errors = []
    version = manifest_version(root)
    texts = {}
    for name in ("README.md", "DESCRIPTION.md", "CHANGELOG.md"):
        p = root / name
        texts[name] = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if p.is_file() else None
    if texts["README.md"] is not None:
        check_project_readme(texts["README.md"], errors)
    if texts["DESCRIPTION.md"] is not None:
        check_description_badges(texts["DESCRIPTION.md"], errors)
        check_description(texts["DESCRIPTION.md"], version, errors, ptype, images_dir)
    if texts["CHANGELOG.md"] is not None:
        check_changelog(texts["CHANGELOG.md"], version, errors)
    seen, unique = set(), []
    for e in errors:
        if e not in seen:
            seen.add(e)
            unique.append(e)
    return unique


def compliant_docs(ptype, name, version, ext=None, date="2026-01-01"):
    ext = ext or {"addon": "mcaddon", "map": "mcworld", "script": "mcpack", "minigame": "mcaddon"}.get(ptype, "mcaddon")
    cards = "\n".join(f"### Feature {i}\n- **What Happens**: Players can use feature {i} in the world.\n" for i in range(1, 9))
    achievement = "Likely achievement-friendly." if ptype == "addon" else "Unknown / needs confirmation."
    description = (f"# {name} — A clear factual subtitle\n\n**By ALNStudios**\n\n"
                   f"{DESCRIPTION_BADGES[0]}\n{DESCRIPTION_BADGES[1]}\n\n"
                   f"**A short player-facing tagline.**\n\n> A concise, factual pitch explains what players can do and what makes the experience distinct.\n\n"
                   f"## Overview\n\nThis creation adds a small experience to your world.\n\n## Key Features\n\n{cards}\n"
                   f"## How to Use\n\nUse the items and interactions provided in the world.\n\n## Compatibility\n\n"
                   f"- Minecraft Bedrock Edition: 1.21.0 or newer.\n- Experimental features: Not verified.\n"
                   f"- Achievements: {achievement}\n- Verification: Source created, not tested in game.\n\n"
                   f"## Creator and Permissions\n\n- Creator: ALNStudios\n- Profiles: [CurseForge Profile]({CF_URL}) · [MCPEDL Profile]({MP_URL})\n\n"
                   f"{LISTING_LINE}\n\n"
                   f"{GUIDELINES}## Search & Discovery Keywords\n\nMinecraft Bedrock add-on, {name} Bedrock mod, MCPEDL add-on, CurseForge Bedrock, ALNStudios.\n")
    base = "https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/tree/main"
    slug = name.replace(" ", "-")
    category = {"addon": "Addons", "map": "Maps", "script": "Script", "minigame": "Minigame"}.get(ptype, "Addons")
    readme = (f"# {name}\n\nDownload the current release of **{name}** for Minecraft Bedrock Edition.\n\n## Download\n\n"
              f"- **Package format:** `.{ext}`\n"
              f"- **Direct download:** [Download {name} (.{ext})]({base}/MCPEDL/{category}/README.md)\n\n"
              f"## Installation\n\n" + "\n".join(
                  INSTALL_STEPS[:2] + (
                      ("3. Find the imported world in your Worlds list and open it.",)
                      if ext == "mcworld" else INSTALL_STEPS[2:]
                  )
              ) + "\n\n## Pack Information\n\n"
              f"For gameplay features, usage details, compatibility notes, and other project information, read the [full description]({base}/MCPEDL/{category}/{slug}/DESCRIPTION.md).\n")
    changelog = (f"# Changelog — {name}\n\n---\n\n## [{version}] - {date} - First Release\n\n### Added\n- **First release**: Players can use the new creation.\n")
    return {"README.md": readme, "DESCRIPTION.md": description, "CHANGELOG.md": changelog}


STYLE_PHRASES = (
    "game-changer", "game changer", "next level", "dive into", "delve", "unleash", "elevate", "elevates",
    "immersive", "seamless", "seamlessly", "revolutionize", "revolutionary", "ultimate", "unparalleled",
    "cutting-edge", "endless possibilities", "world of possibilities", "like never before", "whole new",
    "embark", "journey", "tapestry", "supercharge", "meticulously", "breathe new life", "perfect for",
    "whether you're", "whether you are", "enhance your", "enhances your", "transform your", "take your",
    "adds items to the game", "adds new items", "adds new features", "changes the vanilla game",
    "enhances gameplay", "improves gameplay", "a variety of", "various",
)


def style_warnings(text, name="DESCRIPTION.md"):
    hits = []
    lines = norm(text).split("\n")
    for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        low = line.lower()
        for phrase in STYLE_PHRASES:
            if re.search(r"\b" + re.escape(phrase) + r"\b", low):
                hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} line {i} uses '{phrase}'. Replace it with a specific fact about what players can do.")
        if "!" in line.replace("![", ""):
            hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} line {i} has an exclamation mark. State the fact plainly.")
    dashes = sum(l.count("\u2014") for l in lines[1:] if not l.startswith("#"))
    if dashes > 3:
        hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} has {dashes} em dashes outside headings. Use plain sentences instead.")
    for block in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", norm(text)):
        b = block.strip()
        if not b or b[0] in "#->*<[|" or b[0].isdigit():
            continue
        if len(re.findall(r"[.!?](?:\s|$)", b)) > 4:
            hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} has a paragraph of more than four sentences: '{b[:50]}...'. Split or shorten it.")
    tags = [l for l in lines if re.match(r"^\*\*[^*].*\*\*$", l.strip()) and "By ALNStudios" not in l]
    if not tags:
        hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} has no bold tagline. Add one concrete sentence of 25 words or fewer.")
    elif len(tags[0].strip("*").split()) > 25:
        hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} tagline is longer than 25 words. It should work as the CurseForge Summary.")
    elif not re.search(r"\d|[A-Z][a-z]+ [A-Z]|[A-Z][a-z]+, [A-Z]", tags[0].strip("*")[1:]):
        hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} tagline names no number or specific item. Add one verified detail.")
    howto = re.search(r"(?ms)^## How to Use\n(.*?)(?=^## |\Z)", norm(text))
    if howto and not re.search(r"(?m)^\d+\. ", howto.group(1)):
        hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} How to Use has no numbered steps.")
    for card in re.findall(r"(?m)^### (.+)$", norm(text)):
        if card.strip().lower() in ("features", "overview", "benefits", "gameplay", "items", "improvements", "extras"):
            hits.append(f"STYLE: {name} feature card '{card}' is too generic. Name the actual mechanic or item.")
    return hits


def style_audit(targets):
    total = 0
    for ptype, path in targets:
        f = Path(path) / "DESCRIPTION.md"
        if not f.is_file():
            continue
        hits = style_warnings(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        rel = Path(path)
        try:
            rel = rel.relative_to(ROOT)
        except ValueError:
            pass
        print(("style " if hits else "ok    ") + rel.as_posix())
        for h in hits:
            print("  - " + h)
        total += bool(hits)
    print(f"[Style] {total} description(s) with style hints (advisory, not build failures).")
    return 0


def audit(targets):
    bad = 0
    for ptype, path in targets:
        errors = check_docs(path, ptype)
        rel = Path(path)
        try:
            rel = rel.relative_to(ROOT)
        except ValueError:
            pass
        if errors:
            bad += 1
            print(f"FAIL {rel.as_posix()} ({ptype})")
            for e in errors:
                print(f"  - {e}")
        else:
            print(f"ok   {rel.as_posix()} ({ptype})")
    return bad


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("path", nargs="?", help="A folder to audit (payload folder or project folder).")
    parser.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="Every project under MCPEDL/.")
    parser.add_argument("--project", help="One project folder name under MCPEDL/.")
    parser.add_argument("--type", choices=("addon", "map", "script", "minigame"), help="Project type for a PATH audit (default: addon).")
    parser.add_argument("--style", action="store_true", help="Advisory style hints for DESCRIPTION.md (filler phrases, long paragraphs).")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    kinds = {"Addons": "addon", "Maps": "map", "Script": "script", "Minigame": "minigame"}
    targets = []
    if args.path:
        targets.append((args.type or "addon", Path(args.path)))
    else:
        for cat, ptype in kinds.items():
            base = ROOT / "MCPEDL" / cat
            if base.is_dir():
                for p in sorted(x for x in base.iterdir() if x.is_dir()):
                    if args.all or (args.project and args.project in (p.name, f"MCPEDL/{cat}/{p.name}")):
                        targets.append((ptype, p))
    if not targets:
        print("Nothing to audit: pass --all, --project NAME, or a PATH.", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if args.style:
        return style_audit(targets)
    bad = audit(targets)
    print(f"[Docs] {len(targets)} project(s) audited, {bad} not following the template.")
    return 1 if bad else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
````

## FILE: scripts/pixel_json_to_png.py (243 lines, 11036 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Turn pixel JSON into PNG textures. Standard library only (no Pillow), so it runs in Termux, GitHub and any AI sandbox.

Source: <project>/texture_sources/*.json (never inside resource_pack/). Each file holds an array of textures
(a single object, or {"textures": [...]}, also works):

  {
    "name": "shepherds_pie",              lowercase letters, digits, underscore; becomes <name>.png
    "size": 16,                           16, 32, 64 or 128; pixels must be exactly size x size
    "folder": "items",                    optional: items (default), blocks, or items/<sub> e.g. items/banquet
    "palette": {"0": "#00000000", "1": "#3d2817"},   index -> #RRGGBB or #RRGGBBAA
    "pixels": [[0, 0, 1, ...], ...]       size rows, each with size palette indexes
  }

Output: <project>/resource_pack/textures/<folder>/<name>.png (nearest-neighbor exact pixels, same JSON = same PNG).
Every problem in every file is reported together; nothing is written unless all textures are valid.

Usage
  python3 scripts/pixel_json_to_png.py MCPEDL/Addons/<pack>     convert (also works on a build/<Name> scratch folder)
  python3 scripts/pixel_json_to_png.py <project> --check        validate only
  python3 scripts/pixel_json_to_png.py <project> --preview [FILE]   also write a contact sheet PNG to FILE (default
                                                                 ./pixel_preview.png, OUTSIDE the project so it never ships)
  python3 scripts/pixel_json_to_png.py <project> --input FILE   use this JSON file instead of texture_sources/*.json
Edit the JSON, never the PNG: the PNG is regenerated from it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import struct
import sys
import zlib
from pathlib import Path

SIZES = (16, 32, 64, 128)
NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9_]+$")
FOLDER_RE = re.compile(r"^(items|blocks)(/[a-z0-9_]+)*$")
HEX_RE = re.compile(r"^#([0-9a-fA-F]{6})([0-9a-fA-F]{2})?$")
SOURCE_DIR = "texture_sources"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- PNG writer (RGBA, 8 bit, filter 0)
def _chunk(tag: bytes, data: bytes) -> bytes:
    return struct.pack(">I", len(data)) + tag + data + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(tag + data) & 0xFFFFFFFF)


def encode_png(width: int, height: int, rgba_rows: list[bytes]) -> bytes:
    raw = b"".join(b"\x00" + row for row in rgba_rows)
    return (b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
            + _chunk(b"IHDR", struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", width, height, 8, 6, 0, 0, 0))
            + _chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(raw, 9))
            + _chunk(b"IEND", b""))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- validation
def parse_color(value) -> tuple[int, int, int, int] | None:
    m = HEX_RE.match(value) if isinstance(value, str) else None
    if not m:
        return None
    rgb = m.group(1)
    alpha = int(m.group(2), 16) if m.group(2) else 255
    return int(rgb[0:2], 16), int(rgb[2:4], 16), int(rgb[4:6], 16), alpha


def textures_in(doc):
    if isinstance(doc, dict) and isinstance(doc.get("textures"), list):
        return doc["textures"]
    if isinstance(doc, dict):
        return [doc]
    return doc if isinstance(doc, list) else None


def validate(tex, where: str) -> tuple[list[str], dict | None]:
    """-> (problems, normalized texture). normalized = name, folder, size, rows (list of list of RGBA tuples), notes."""
    errs: list[str] = []
    if not isinstance(tex, dict):
        return [f"{where}: each texture must be an object"], None
    name = tex.get("name")
    where = f"{where} '{name}'" if isinstance(name, str) else where
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not NAME_RE.match(name):
        errs.append(f"{where}: name must be lowercase letters, digits and underscore only")
    size = tex.get("size")
    if size not in SIZES or isinstance(size, bool):
        errs.append(f"{where}: size must be one of {', '.join(map(str, SIZES))}")
    folder = tex.get("folder", "items")
    if not isinstance(folder, str) or not FOLDER_RE.match(folder):
        errs.append(f"{where}: folder must be items, blocks or items/<name> (lowercase)")
    palette = tex.get("palette")
    colors: dict[int, tuple] = {}
    if not isinstance(palette, dict) or not palette:
        errs.append(f"{where}: palette must be an object like {{\"0\": \"#00000000\"}}")
    else:
        for key, value in palette.items():
            if not (isinstance(key, str) and key.isdigit()):
                errs.append(f"{where}: palette key {key!r} must be a whole number in quotes")
                continue
            rgba = parse_color(value)
            if rgba is None:
                errs.append(f"{where}: palette {key} = {value!r} is not #RRGGBB or #RRGGBBAA")
            else:
                colors[int(key)] = rgba
    pixels = tex.get("pixels")
    rows: list[list[tuple]] = []
    used: set[int] = set()
    if not isinstance(pixels, list):
        errs.append(f"{where}: pixels must be a list of rows")
    elif size in SIZES:
        if len(pixels) != size:
            errs.append(f"{where}: needs {size} rows, found {len(pixels)}")
        for y, row in enumerate(pixels):
            if not isinstance(row, list) or len(row) != size:
                errs.append(f"{where}: row {y} needs {size} values, found {len(row) if isinstance(row, list) else 'a non-list'}")
                continue
            out = []
            for x, idx in enumerate(row):
                if isinstance(idx, str) and idx.isdigit():
                    idx = int(idx)
                if isinstance(idx, bool) or not isinstance(idx, int) or idx not in colors:
                    errs.append(f"{where}: pixel ({x},{y}) uses {row[x]!r}, which is not in the palette")
                    out.append((0, 0, 0, 0))
                else:
                    used.add(idx)
                    out.append(colors[idx])
            rows.append(out)
    if errs:
        return errs, None
    if all(px[3] == 0 for r in rows for px in r):
        return [f"{where}: every pixel is transparent"], None
    unused = sorted(set(colors) - used)
    return [], {"name": name, "folder": folder, "size": size, "rows": rows,
                "notes": [f"{where}: unused palette entries {unused}"] if unused else []}


def load_all(sources: list[Path]):
    """-> (textures, problems, notes) across every source file."""
    textures, problems, notes, seen = [], [], [], {}
    for src in sources:
        label = src.name
        try:
            doc = json.loads(src.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
            problems.append(f"{label}: cannot read JSON ({exc})")
            continue
        items = textures_in(doc)
        if items is None:
            problems.append(f"{label}: expected an array of textures")
            continue
        for i, tex in enumerate(items):
            errs, norm = validate(tex, f"{label}[{i}]")
            problems += errs
            if norm is None:
                continue
            key = (norm["folder"], norm["name"])
            if key in seen:
                problems.append(f"{label}[{i}] '{norm['name']}': duplicate of {seen[key]} (same folder and name)")
                continue
            seen[key] = f"{label}[{i}]"
            notes += norm["notes"]
            textures.append(norm)
    return textures, problems, notes


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- output
def to_png(tex: dict) -> bytes:
    rows = [b"".join(bytes(px) for px in row) for row in tex["rows"]]
    return encode_png(tex["size"], tex["size"], rows)


def preview_png(textures: list[dict], per_row: int = 6, tile: int = 128, gap: int = 12) -> bytes:
    """Contact sheet: every texture scaled to ~tile px over a light/dark checkerboard, for quick human review."""
    cols = min(per_row, max(1, len(textures)))
    rows_n = -(-len(textures) // cols)
    w, h = gap + cols * (tile + gap), gap + rows_n * (tile + gap)
    canvas = [[(38, 38, 42, 255)] * w for _ in range(h)]
    for n, tex in enumerate(textures):
        ox, oy = gap + (n % cols) * (tile + gap), gap + (n // cols) * (tile + gap)
        scale = max(1, tile // tex["size"])
        side = tex["size"] * scale
        for y in range(side):
            for x in range(side):
                r, g, b, a = tex["rows"][y // scale][x // scale]
                bg = 200 if ((x // 8) + (y // 8)) % 2 == 0 else 150
                canvas[oy + y][ox + x] = ((r * a + bg * (255 - a)) // 255, (g * a + bg * (255 - a)) // 255,
                                          (b * a + bg * (255 - a)) // 255, 255)
    return encode_png(w, h, [b"".join(bytes(px) for px in row) for row in canvas])


def find_sources(project: Path, explicit: str | None) -> list[Path]:
    if explicit:
        return [Path(explicit)]
    d = project / SOURCE_DIR
    return sorted(p for p in d.glob("*.json")) if d.is_dir() else []


def run(project: Path, *, check: bool = False, preview: Path | None = None, explicit: str | None = None, log=print) -> int:
    sources = find_sources(project, explicit)
    if not sources:
        log(f"No pixel JSON found (expected {project / SOURCE_DIR}/*.json).")
        return 0
    textures, problems, notes = load_all(sources)
    for n in notes:
        log(f"note   {n}")
    for p in problems:
        log(f"FAIL   {p}")
    if problems:
        log(f"Nothing written: {len(problems)} problem(s) above.")
        return 1
    if check:
        log(f"ok     {len(textures)} texture(s) valid")
        return 0
    base = project / "resource_pack" / "textures"
    for tex in textures:
        target = base / tex["folder"] / f"{tex['name']}.png"
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_bytes(to_png(tex))
        log(f"wrote  {target.relative_to(project).as_posix()}  ({tex['size']}x{tex['size']})")
    if preview:
        Path(preview).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        Path(preview).write_bytes(preview_png(textures))
        log(f"wrote  {preview}  (review sheet, not part of the pack)")
    log(f"PIXEL TEXTURES OK: {len(textures)} PNG(s)")
    return 0


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project", help="pack folder (repo pack or build/<Name>)")
    ap.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="validate only, write nothing")
    ap.add_argument("--preview", nargs="?", const="pixel_preview.png", metavar="FILE",
                    help="also write a contact sheet PNG (default ./pixel_preview.png, outside the project)")
    ap.add_argument("--input", help="use this JSON file instead of texture_sources/*.json")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    project = Path(a.project)
    if not project.is_dir():
        print(f"Project folder not found: {project}")
        return 1
    return run(project, check=a.check, preview=Path(a.preview) if a.preview else None, explicit=a.input)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

## FILE: scripts/make_pack_script.py (250 lines, 10078 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pack script builder for ALNStudios/MCPE-Curseforges.

Turns build/<Project-Name>/ into ONE Termux .sh file that carries the whole project inside it
(as a base64 block at the end). Nothing else is written or delivered: no .zip, .mcaddon, .mcworld,
.mcpack, .json, .js or .b64 file.

It runs the same checks as scripts/build_payload.py, builds the ZIP in memory, wraps it, then proves
the round trip with the importer's own reader.

Usage:  python3 make_pack_script.py build/Project-Name [--rand k7q2x] [--out-dir .] [--check-only]
Output: <Project-Name>_<Version>_<Rand>.sh  (Version = the Behavior Pack version, Rand = 6 random characters)

Run the .sh:
  bash <file>.sh          Termux: creates the project, builds worlds, commits and pushes
  bash <file>.sh stage    Termux: only copies the .sh into MCPEDL/incoming/ and pushes, then run the GitHub
                          workflow "Create Project from Base64" with payload_file = <file>.sh
The workflow only READS the payload block of a .sh. It never executes the file.
Exit codes: 0 ok, 1 checks failed, 2 packaging or round-trip failed.
"""
import argparse
import contextlib
import io
import json
import re
import secrets
import shutil
import string
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import build_payload as bp  # noqa: E402
import import_project_payload as ipp  # noqa: E402

BEGIN, END = ipp.SH_BEGIN, ipp.SH_END
REPO_URL = "https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges.git"

TEMPLATE = r'''#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
# ALNStudios pack script: creates @@DEST@@ from the payload stored at the end of this file.
#   bash @@SCRIPT@@          create the project here (Termux), build worlds, commit and push
#   bash @@SCRIPT@@ stage    copy this file to MCPEDL/incoming/ and push, then run the GitHub workflow
#                            "Create Project from Base64" with payload_file = @@SCRIPT@@
set -e
SELF="$(readlink -f -- "$0")"
SCRIPT_NAME="@@SCRIPT@@"
NAME="@@NAME@@"
DISPLAY_NAME='@@DISPLAY@@'
VERSION="@@VERSION@@"
PTYPE="@@PTYPE@@"
REPO="@@REPO@@"
ACTION="${1:-create}"
case "$ACTION" in create|stage) ;; *) echo "Usage: bash $SCRIPT_NAME [create|stage]"; exit 1 ;; esac

cd "$HOME"
[ -d "$HOME/MCPE-Curseforges" ] || git clone "$REPO" "$HOME/MCPE-Curseforges"
cd "$HOME/MCPE-Curseforges"
git checkout -q -- Package changelog_views 2>/dev/null || true
git pull --rebase --autostash

for f in scripts/import_project_payload.py scripts/build_payload.py scripts/manifest_tools.py; do
  [ -f "$f" ] || { echo "ERROR: $f is missing in the repo."; exit 1; }
done

if git ls-tree -r --name-only HEAD | grep -q "^MCPEDL/[^/]*/$NAME/"; then
  echo "Nothing to commit: $NAME already exists in the repo."
  rm -f -- "$SELF"
  exit 0
fi

if [ "$ACTION" = "stage" ]; then
  grep -q "ALN PAYLOAD BEGIN" scripts/import_project_payload.py \
    || { echo "ERROR: the repo importer cannot read .sh files yet. Update the repo first."; exit 1; }
  mkdir -p MCPEDL/incoming
  cp -- "$SELF" "MCPEDL/incoming/$SCRIPT_NAME"
  git add -- "MCPEDL/incoming/$SCRIPT_NAME"
  git diff --cached --quiet || git commit -m "chore(incoming): stage $NAME pack script"
  git push
  echo "Staged. Now: GitHub > Actions > Create Project from Base64 > Run workflow > payload_file = $SCRIPT_NAME"
  rm -f -- "$SELF"
  exit 0
fi

mkdir -p MCPEDL/incoming
TMPB64="MCPEDL/incoming/$NAME.b64"
GO="$(mktemp)"
DONE=0
CREATED=0
DEST_REAL="MCPEDL/@@DESTDIR@@/$NAME"
cleanup() {
  rc=$?
  rm -f -- "$TMPB64" "$GO"
  if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ] && [ "$DONE" != "1" ]; then
    git checkout -q -- . 2>/dev/null || true
    [ "$CREATED" = "1" ] && rm -rf -- "$DEST_REAL"
    echo "Failed. Nothing was pushed."
  fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT

sed -n '/^#--- ALN PAYLOAD BEGIN ---$/,/^#--- ALN PAYLOAD END ---$/p' "$SELF" | sed '1d;$d' | sed 's/^#//' | tr -d ' \r\n' > "$TMPB64"
[ -s "$TMPB64" ] || { echo "ERROR: the payload block is missing from this file."; exit 1; }

CREATED=1
python3 scripts/import_project_payload.py --mode create --payload-file "$NAME.b64" --github-output "$GO"
TYPE="$(sed -n 's/^type=//p' "$GO")"
DEST_REAL="$(sed -n 's/^dest=//p' "$GO")"

if [ "$TYPE" = "map" ]; then
  for m in Creative Survival Hardcore; do
    [ -f "Mcpeworlds/$m/level.dat" ] || { echo "ERROR: missing Mcpeworlds/$m/level.dat"; exit 1; }
  done
  python3 scripts/build_oneblock_variants_from_template.py
  python3 scripts/sync_oneblock_map_readmes.py
fi
python3 scripts/manifest_tools.py --project "$DEST_REAL" --align
python3 scripts/validate_manifest_titles.py --project "$DEST_REAL"
python3 scripts/build_listing_kits.py --project "$DEST_REAL" 2>/dev/null || true
python3 scripts/build_changelog_views.py --project "$NAME" 2>/dev/null || true

NAME="$NAME" DISPLAY_NAME="$DISPLAY_NAME" TYPE="$TYPE" python3 - <<'ALN_LOG'
import os, pathlib, subprocess
date = subprocess.check_output(["date", "+%F"], env={"TZ": "Asia/Singapore", "PATH": "/usr/bin:/bin:/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin"}, text=True).strip()
title = f"New {os.environ['TYPE'].capitalize()} {os.environ['DISPLAY_NAME']}"
p = pathlib.Path("CHANGELOG.md")
t = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
if f"## {date} - {title}" not in t:
    entry = f"## {date} - {title}\n\n### Added\n- **{os.environ['DISPLAY_NAME']}**: New {os.environ['TYPE']} project created from a pack script.\n\n"
    i = t.index("---\n") + 4
    p.write_text(t[:i] + "\n" + entry.rstrip("\n") + "\n" + t[i:], encoding="utf-8")
ALN_LOG

git add -A -- "$DEST_REAL" CHANGELOG.md Package/ changelog_views/
if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  echo "Nothing to commit"
else
  git commit -m "feat($TYPE): create $NAME from pack script"
  git push
fi
DONE=1
rm -f -- "$SELF"
exit 0
@@PAYLOAD@@
'''


def fail(msg, code=1):
    print(msg)
    sys.exit(code)


def version_of(root: Path) -> str:
    try:
        v = json.loads((root / "behavior_pack/manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))["header"]["version"]
        if bp_is_triple(v):
            return ".".join(str(p) for p in v)
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
        pass
    return "1.0.0"


def bp_is_triple(v):
    return isinstance(v, list) and len(v) == 3 and all(type(p) is int and p >= 0 for p in v)


def build_zip_b64(root: Path) -> str:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        out = Path(tmp) / "p.b64"
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
            bp.pack(root, out)
        return out.read_text(encoding="ascii")


def wrap(b64: str, width=100) -> str:
    return "\n".join("#" + b64[i:i + width] for i in range(0, len(b64), width))


def make(root: Path, out_dir: Path, rand: str) -> Path:
    pj = bp.read_project(root)
    files = {p.relative_to(root).as_posix(): p.read_bytes()
             for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    name = ipp.name_from_project_json(files)
    if not bp.NAME_RE.match(name):
        fail("Name: 1-64 chars, letters/numbers/. _ - only, starting with a letter or number.")
    version = version_of(root)
    display = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9 .&_-]", "", str(pj.get("name", name))).strip() or name
    script = f"{name}_{version}_{rand}.sh"
    b64 = build_zip_b64(root)
    body = (TEMPLATE.replace("@@SCRIPT@@", script).replace("@@NAME@@", name).replace("@@DISPLAY@@", display)
            .replace("@@VERSION@@", version).replace("@@PTYPE@@", pj["type"]).replace("@@REPO@@", REPO_URL)
            .replace("@@DESTDIR@@", bp.DEST_DIR[pj["type"]]).replace("@@DEST@@", f"MCPEDL/{bp.DEST_DIR[pj['type']]}/{name}")
            .replace("@@PAYLOAD@@", BEGIN + "\n" + wrap(b64) + "\n" + END))
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = out_dir / script
    target.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    back = ipp.payload_from_text(target.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), target.name)
    if back != b64:
        target.unlink()
        fail("ROUND-TRIP FAILED: the payload read back from the .sh differs.", 2)
    z = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(__import__("base64").b64decode(back, validate=True)))
    if z.testzip() is not None or "project.json" not in z.namelist():
        target.unlink()
        fail("ROUND-TRIP FAILED: the ZIP inside the .sh is damaged.", 2)
    if shutil.which("bash"):
        r = subprocess.run(["bash", "-n", str(target)], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            target.unlink()
            fail("SYNTAX CHECK FAILED: " + r.stderr, 2)
    print(f"PACK SCRIPT OK: {script} ({target.stat().st_size} bytes, {len(z.namelist())} files inside). "
          "Structure only: not tested in game.")
    return target


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("project", help="e.g. build/Project-Name")
    ap.add_argument("--rand", help="at least 5 lowercase letters or digits (default: random)")
    ap.add_argument("--out-dir", default=".")
    ap.add_argument("--check-only", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    root = Path(a.project)
    if not root.is_dir():
        fail(f"Project folder not found: {root}")
    if bp.read_project(root) is None:
        fail('Missing or invalid project.json. Create it first: {"type": "addon|map|script|minigame", "name": "Display Name"}')
    rand = a.rand or "".join(secrets.choice(string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits) for _ in range(6))
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9]{5,}", rand):
        fail("--rand needs at least 5 lowercase letters or digits.")
    bp.install_assets(root)
    fails, warns, n, total = bp.run_checks(root)
    for w in sorted(set(warns)):
        print("WARNING:", w)
    fails = [f for f in fails if f]
    if fails:
        print("SELF-TEST FAILED")
        for f in sorted(set(fails)):
            print(" -", f)
        sys.exit(1)
    print(f"SELF-TEST PASSED ({n} files, {total} bytes)")
    if not a.check_only:
        make(root, Path(a.out_dir), rand)
        print("BUILD OK. Structure only: not tested in game.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

## FILE: scripts/make_update_script.py (167 lines, 6732 bytes)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Update script builder for ALNStudios/MCPE-Curseforges.

Turns a search-and-replace spec (format: see scripts/apply_update.py) into ONE Termux .sh named
<Repofilename>_<Version>_<Rand>.sh. The spec travels inside the .sh as a comment block, so the file is
small, readable and the only thing delivered. The .sh pulls, applies the edits with apply_update.py
(exact unique match, all-or-nothing, idempotent), sets the manifest version, writes both changelogs,
commits and pushes.

File removal: a line "@@ delete-file <path or glob>" (repo-root-relative, tracked files only, * does not cross a
folder, e.g. "scripts/old.py" or "MCPEDL/*/*/test_world.json") makes the .sh run "git rm" on every match. The
line is taken out of the spec before apply_update.py reads it, so older copies of apply_update.py still work.

Usage: python3 make_update_script.py spec.txt [--rand k7q2x] [--out-dir .] [--verify PATH_TO_REPO_CLONE]
  --verify  dry-runs the spec against a local clone and refuses to build if any edit would fail.
Run the .sh:  bash ~/storage/downloads/<file>.sh
"""
import argparse
import re
import secrets
import shutil
import string
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import apply_update as au  # noqa: E402

BEGIN, END = "#--- ALN UPDATE BEGIN ---", "#--- ALN UPDATE END ---"
DELETE = "@@ delete-file "
DELETE_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._][A-Za-z0-9._ */-]*$")
REPO_URL = "https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges.git"

TEMPLATE = r'''#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
# ALNStudios update script: @@PROJECT@@ -> v@@VERSION@@ (search and replace edits stored at the end of this file).
#   bash @@SCRIPT@@
set -e
SELF="$(readlink -f -- "$0")"
SCRIPT_NAME="@@SCRIPT@@"
REPO="@@REPO@@"
VERSION="@@VERSION@@"
DEST="@@PROJECT@@"
cd "$HOME"
[ -d "$HOME/MCPE-Curseforges" ] || git clone "$REPO" "$HOME/MCPE-Curseforges"
cd "$HOME/MCPE-Curseforges"
git checkout -q -- Package changelog_views 2>/dev/null || true
git pull --rebase --autostash
[ -f scripts/apply_update.py ] || { echo "ERROR: scripts/apply_update.py is missing in the repo. Apply the Search-Replace-Update script first."; exit 1; }

SPEC="$(mktemp)"
EDITS="$(mktemp)"
DELETES="$(mktemp)"
TOUCHED="$(mktemp)"
DONE=0
cleanup() {
  rc=$?
  rm -f -- "$SPEC" "$EDITS" "$DELETES" "$TOUCHED"
  if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ] && [ "$DONE" != "1" ]; then
    git reset -q 2>/dev/null || true
    git checkout -q -- . 2>/dev/null || true
    [ "$DEST" != "." ] && git clean -fdq -- "$DEST" 2>/dev/null || true
    echo "Failed. Nothing was pushed."
  fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT

sed -n '/^#--- ALN UPDATE BEGIN ---$/,/^#--- ALN UPDATE END ---$/p' "$SELF" | sed '1d;$d' | sed 's/^#//' > "$SPEC"
[ -s "$SPEC" ] || { echo "ERROR: the edit block is missing from this file."; exit 1; }

grep '^@@ delete-file ' "$SPEC" | sed 's/^@@ delete-file //' > "$DELETES" || true
grep -v '^@@ delete-file ' "$SPEC" > "$EDITS" || true

DATE="$(TZ=Asia/Singapore date +%F)"
python3 scripts/apply_update.py "$EDITS" --date "$DATE" --touched "$TOUCHED"

if git diff --name-only | grep -q "INSTRUCTIONS.md"; then
  python3 scripts/check_instruction_files.py
fi

while IFS= read -r pat; do
  [ -n "$pat" ] || continue
  found=0
  while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
    git rm -q -f -- "$f"
    echo "deleted  $f"
    found=1
  done < <(git ls-files -z -- ":(glob)$pat")
  [ "$found" = "1" ] || echo "skipped  $pat (no tracked file matches)"
done < "$DELETES"

while IFS= read -r f; do
  [ -n "$f" ] && git add -- "$f"
done < "$TOUCHED"
if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  echo "Nothing to commit"
else
  git commit -m "chore: update $DEST to v$VERSION"
  git push
fi
DONE=1
rm -f -- "$SELF"
exit 0
@@BLOCK@@
'''


def fail(msg, code=1):
    print(msg)
    sys.exit(code)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("spec")
    ap.add_argument("--rand")
    ap.add_argument("--out-dir", default=".")
    ap.add_argument("--verify", help="path of a local clone of the repo to dry-run against")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    text = Path(a.spec).read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace("\r\n", "\n")
    deletes = [l[len(DELETE):].strip() for l in text.split("\n") if l.startswith(DELETE)]
    for d in deletes:
        if not DELETE_RE.match(d) or ".." in d.split("/") or ":" in d:
            fail(f"SPEC ERROR: unsafe delete-file path: {d!r}")
    try:
        spec = au.parse("\n".join(l for l in text.split("\n") if not l.startswith(DELETE)))
    except au.Fail as e:
        fail(f"SPEC ERROR: {e}")
    rand = a.rand or "".join(secrets.choice(string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits) for _ in range(6))
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9]{5,}", rand):
        fail("--rand needs at least 5 lowercase letters or digits.")
    proj = spec["project"].strip().rstrip("/")
    name = "Repo" if proj == "." else proj.split("/")[-1]
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,64}", name):
        fail(f"Project folder name not usable in a file name: {name}")
    if not spec["ops"] and not spec["changelog"] and not deletes:
        fail("SPEC ERROR: no edits and no changelog.")
    if proj != "." and not spec["changelog"]:
        fail("SPEC ERROR: a project update needs '@@ changelog <Update Name>' (CHANGELOG.md is mandatory).")
    if a.verify:
        try:
            notes, touched = au.run(spec, a.verify, "2000-01-01", dry=True)
        except au.Fail as e:
            fail(f"VERIFY FAILED: {e}", e.code)
        print(f"VERIFY OK (dry run): {len(touched)} file(s) would change")
    label = f"v{spec['version']}" if proj == "." and spec["version"].isdigit() else spec["version"]
    script = f"{name}_{label}_{rand}.sh"
    block = BEGIN + "\n" + "\n".join("#" + l for l in text.rstrip("\n").split("\n")) + "\n" + END
    body = (TEMPLATE.replace("@@SCRIPT@@", script).replace("@@VERSION@@", spec["version"])
            .replace("@@PROJECT@@", proj).replace("@@REPO@@", REPO_URL).replace("@@BLOCK@@", block))
    out = Path(a.out_dir)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = out / script
    target.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    got = "\n".join(l[1:] for l in body.split(BEGIN + "\n", 1)[1].split("\n" + END, 1)[0].split("\n"))
    if got != text.rstrip("\n"):
        target.unlink()
        fail("ROUND-TRIP FAILED: the edit block read back from the .sh differs.", 2)
    if shutil.which("bash") and subprocess.run(["bash", "-n", str(target)]).returncode != 0:
        target.unlink()
        fail("SYNTAX CHECK FAILED", 2)
    print(f"UPDATE SCRIPT OK: {script} ({len(spec['ops'])} edit(s)). Not tested in game.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## FILE: scripts/apply_update.py (339 lines, 15060 bytes)

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Search-and-replace updater for ALNStudios/MCPE-Curseforges (runs inside every update .sh).

Applies a small text spec to a project instead of rewriting whole files. All-or-nothing: every edit is
checked in memory first, nothing is written if any edit fails. Safe to run twice (applied edits are skipped).

Spec format (lines starting with "@@ " are directives; a content line that must start with "@@" is written
"@@@"; the first "@" is removed):
  @@ version 1.2.4                      new X.Y.Z (must be the current highest + 0.0.1, or already applied)
                                        repo level (@@ project .): just the number, e.g. @@ version 80 (= REPO_VERSION + 1)
  @@ project MCPEDL/Addons/Mining-Helmet   folder every @@ file path is relative to ("." = repo root, no version step)
  @@ file behavior_pack/scripts/main.js
  @@ replace                            exact text that must occur ONCE (partial lines are fine)
  old text
  @@ with
  new text
  @@ replace-all ... @@ with ...        every occurrence
  @@ insert-after / @@ insert-before    anchor line(s) occurring once, then @@ with + the lines to insert
  @@ delete                             exact text occurring once (whole lines remove their newline too)
  @@ append                             text added to the end (skipped when already there)
  @@ create                             new file (error when it exists with other content)
  @@ overwrite                          whole-file replacement (only for new content over 60% of the file)
  @@ changelog Update Name              project CHANGELOG.md entry "## [X.Y.Z] - date - Update Name" + body
  @@ root-changelog Update Name         optional; default = same name and body in the root CHANGELOG.md
Block text is the lines between directives, joined with newlines (no trimming). Put no blank line before the
next directive unless the text needs it.

Usage: python3 scripts/apply_update.py SPEC --date YYYY-MM-DD [--root DIR] [--dry-run] [--touched FILE]
Exit: 0 ok, 1 spec or match error, 3 version is stale, 4 validation failed.
"""
import argparse
import json
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

NAMES = {"version", "project", "file", "replace", "replace-all", "insert-after", "insert-before", "delete", "with",
         "append", "create", "overwrite", "changelog", "root-changelog"}
NEEDS_WITH = {"replace", "replace-all", "insert-after", "insert-before"}
VER = re.compile(r"^(?:v\d+|\d+\.\d+\.\d+)$", re.IGNORECASE)
REPO_VER = re.compile(r"^(?:\d+\.\d+\.)?(\d+)$")


class Fail(Exception):
    def __init__(self, msg, code=1):
        super().__init__(msg)
        self.code = code


def parse(text):
    toks = []
    for n, line in enumerate(text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n"), 1):
        if line.startswith("@@@"):
            toks[-1][2].append(line[1:]) if toks else None
        elif line == "@@" or line.startswith("@@ "):
            name, _, arg = line[3:].strip().partition(" ")
            if name not in NAMES:
                raise Fail(f"spec line {n}: unknown directive '@@ {name}'")
            toks.append([name, arg.strip(), []])
        elif toks:
            toks[-1][2].append(line)
        elif line.strip():
            raise Fail(f"spec line {n}: text before the first directive")
    if toks and toks[-1][2] and toks[-1][2][-1] == "":
        toks[-1][2].pop()
    spec = {"version": None, "project": None, "ops": [], "changelog": None, "root": None}
    cur, i = None, 0
    while i < len(toks):
        name, arg, lines = toks[i]
        body = "\n".join(lines)
        i += 1
        if name == "version":
            spec["version"] = arg
        elif name == "project":
            spec["project"] = arg
        elif name == "file":
            cur = arg
        elif name in ("changelog", "root-changelog"):
            spec["changelog" if name == "changelog" else "root"] = (arg or "Update", body)
        elif name == "with":
            raise Fail("'@@ with' without a matching @@ replace, @@ replace-all, @@ insert-after or @@ insert-before")
        else:
            if cur is None:
                raise Fail(f"'@@ {name}' needs a '@@ file <path>' before it")
            new = None
            if name in NEEDS_WITH:
                if i >= len(toks) or toks[i][0] != "with":
                    raise Fail(f"'@@ {name}' on {cur} needs '@@ with'")
                new = "\n".join(toks[i][2])
                i += 1
            spec["ops"].append({"op": name, "file": cur, "old": body, "new": new})
    repo = (spec["project"] or "").strip().rstrip("/") == "."
    if not spec["version"] or not (REPO_VER if repo else VER).match(spec["version"]):
        raise Fail("spec needs '@@ version vX or X.Y.Z' (a repo-level spec uses just the number, '@@ version 80')")
    if not spec["project"]:
        raise Fail("spec needs '@@ project <folder>'")
    return spec


def apply_op(t, o):
    op, old, new = o["op"], o["old"], o["new"]
    if op in ("replace", "replace-all"):
        c = t.count(old) if old else 0
        if not old:
            raise Fail(f"{o['file']}: empty search text")
        if c == 0:
            if new in t:
                return t, False
            raise Fail(f"{o['file']}: text not found: {old.splitlines()[0][:80]!r}")
        if new in t and old in new:
            return t, False
        if op == "replace" and c > 1:
            raise Fail(f"{o['file']}: text found {c} times, add more context or use replace-all: {old.splitlines()[0][:80]!r}")
        return t.replace(old, new), True
    if op in ("insert-after", "insert-before"):
        if new in t and old in t:
            return t, False
        c = t.count(old)
        if c != 1:
            raise Fail(f"{o['file']}: anchor found {c} times (needs exactly 1): {old.splitlines()[0][:80]!r}")
        a = t.index(old)
        if op == "insert-after":
            e = t.find("\n", a + len(old))
            e = len(t) if e < 0 else e
            return t[:e] + "\n" + new + t[e:], True
        s = t.rfind("\n", 0, a) + 1
        return t[:s] + new + "\n" + t[s:], True
    if op == "delete":
        c = t.count(old)
        if c == 0:
            return t, False
        if c > 1:
            raise Fail(f"{o['file']}: delete text found {c} times: {old.splitlines()[0][:80]!r}")
        a = t.index(old)
        e = a + len(old)
        if (a == 0 or t[a - 1] == "\n") and t[e:e + 1] == "\n":
            e += 1
        return t[:a] + t[e:], True
    if op == "append":
        if old.strip("\n") and old.strip("\n") in t:
            return t, False
        return (t if t.endswith("\n") or not t else t + "\n") + old.rstrip("\n") + "\n", True
    raise Fail(f"unknown op {op}")


def vtuple(v):
    cleaned = str(v or "").strip().lstrip("vV")
    parts = [int(p) for p in cleaned.split(".")]
    if len(parts) == 1:
        return (parts[0], 0, 0)
    if len(parts) == 3:
        return (parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
    return tuple(parts)


def current_highest(proj):
    best = None
    for m in proj.rglob("manifest.json"):
        try:
            v = json.loads(m.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))["header"]["version"]
            if isinstance(v, list) and len(v) == 3:
                best = max(best or (0, 0, 0), tuple(v))
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
            pass
    c = proj / "CHANGELOG.md"
    if c.is_file():
        m = re.search(r"(?m)^## \[(?:v)?(\d+(?:\.\d+\.\d+)?)\]", c.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if m:
            best = max(best or (0, 0, 0), vtuple(m.group(1)))
    return best


def add_entry(t, heading, body):
    if heading in t:
        return t, False
    i = t.index("---\n") + 4 if "---\n" in t else len(t)
    return t[:i] + "\n" + heading + "\n\n" + body.strip("\n") + "\n" + t[i:], True


def js_syntax(path):
    """(ran, error). A missing or broken node (for example a Termux library error) is 'not checked', never a syntax error."""
    node = shutil.which("node")
    if not node:
        return False, ""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
        tmp = Path(t) / "check.mjs"
        shutil.copyfile(path, tmp)
        r = subprocess.run([node, "--check", str(tmp)], capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode == 0:
        return True, ""
    if "SyntaxError" not in r.stderr:
        return False, ""
    errs = [ln for ln in r.stderr.splitlines() if "Error" in ln]
    return True, (errs[-1] if errs else "syntax error").strip()


def run(spec, root, date, dry=False):
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    ver = spec["version"]
    project = spec["project"].strip().rstrip("/")
    repo_level = project == "."
    proj = (root / project).resolve()
    if not repo_level and root not in proj.parents:
        raise Fail(f"project path leaves the repo: {project}")
    if not proj.is_dir():
        raise Fail(f"project folder not found: {project}")
    if not repo_level:
        hi = current_highest(proj)
        want = vtuple(ver)
        allowed = [hi] if hi is not None else []
        if hi is not None:
            allowed.append((hi[0], hi[1], hi[2] + 1))
            allowed.append((hi[0] + 1, 0, 0))
        if hi is not None and want not in allowed:
            raise Fail(f"stale script: {project} is at {'.'.join(map(str, hi))}, this script makes {ver}. "
                       "Expected the new version to be the next single version or patch above.", 3)
    files, notes = {}, []
    for o in spec["ops"]:
        rel = o["file"]
        p = (proj / rel).resolve()
        if proj not in p.parents and not (repo_level and root in p.parents):
            raise Fail(f"{rel}: path leaves the project folder")
        if rel.endswith("CHANGELOG.md") and not repo_level:
            raise Fail("edit CHANGELOG.md with @@ changelog, not with a file edit")
        if p not in files:
            files[p] = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if p.is_file() else None
        t = files[p]
        if o["op"] in ("create", "overwrite"):
            new = o["old"].rstrip("\n") + "\n"
            if t == new:
                notes.append(f"skipped  {rel} (already identical)")
                continue
            if t is not None and o["op"] == "create":
                raise Fail(f"{rel}: already exists with other content (use @@ overwrite only for big rewrites)")
            files[p] = new
            notes.append(f"{'created' if t is None else 'rewrote'}  {rel}")
            continue
        if t is None:
            raise Fail(f"{rel}: file not found")
        files[p], changed = apply_op(t, o)
        notes.append(f"{'patched ' if changed else 'skipped '} {rel} ({o['op']}{'' if changed else ', already applied'})")
    if spec["changelog"] and not repo_level:
        c = proj / "CHANGELOG.md"
        if not c.is_file():
            raise Fail(f"{project}/CHANGELOG.md not found")
        name, body = spec["changelog"]
        files[c], ch = add_entry(files.get(c) or c.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), f"## [{ver}] - {date} - {name}", body)
        notes.append(f"{'added   ' if ch else 'skipped '} {project}/CHANGELOG.md entry {ver}")
    rootspec = spec["root"] or spec["changelog"]
    if rootspec:
        rc = root / "CHANGELOG.md"
        name, body = rootspec
        rt = files.get(rc) or rc.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        vp = root / "REPO_VERSION"
        dup = re.search(r"^## \[v?\d+(?:\.\d+\.\d+)?\] - " + re.escape(f"{date} - {name}") + r"$", rt, re.M)
        if dup or f"## {date} - {name}" in rt:
            ch = False
        else:
            cur = vp.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() if vp.is_file() else ""
            old_ver = REPO_VER.match(cur)
            if old_ver:
                cur = str(int(old_ver.group(1)) + 1)
                if repo_level and REPO_VER.match(ver).group(1) != cur:
                    raise Fail(f"stale script: the repo goes to {cur}, but this script is named for {ver}. "
                               f"Use '@@ version {cur}'.", 3)
            else:
                cur = "1"
            files[vp] = cur + "\n"
            rt, ch = add_entry(rt, f"## [v{cur}] - {date} - {name}", body)
            files[rc] = rt
        notes.append(f"{'added   ' if ch else 'skipped '} CHANGELOG.md (root) entry")
    for p, t in files.items():
        if p.suffix == ".json" and t is not None:
            try:
                json.loads(t)
            except ValueError as e:
                raise Fail(f"{p.relative_to(root)}: invalid JSON after the edit: {e}", 4)
    touched = []
    for p, t in files.items():
        if t is None:
            continue
        before = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if p.is_file() else None
        if before != t:
            touched.append(p)
            if not dry:
                p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                p.write_text(t, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    if not dry:
        unchecked = False
        for p in touched:
            if p.suffix in (".js", ".mjs"):
                ran, err = js_syntax(p)
                if err:
                    raise Fail(f"{p.relative_to(root)}: JavaScript syntax error: {err}", 4)
                unchecked = unchecked or not ran
        if unchecked:
            notes.append("warning: node is missing or not usable, JavaScript syntax not checked")
    if not dry and not repo_level and (proj / "behavior_pack/manifest.json").is_file():
        tools = root / "scripts" / "manifest_tools.py"
        for args in (["--set", ver], ["--check"]):
            r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(tools), "--project", str(proj), *args], capture_output=True, text=True)
            if r.returncode != 0:
                raise Fail("manifest_tools " + args[0] + " failed: " + (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip()[-300:], 4)
        for m in proj.rglob("manifest.json"):
            if m not in touched:
                touched.append(m)
    return notes, sorted({p for p in touched})


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("spec")
    ap.add_argument("--date", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--root", default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--touched", help="write the list of changed repo-relative paths to this file")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        spec = parse(Path(a.spec).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        notes, touched = run(spec, a.root, a.date, a.dry_run)
    except Fail as e:
        print("ERROR:", e)
        return e.code
    for n in notes:
        print(n)
    root = Path(a.root).resolve()
    rel = [p.relative_to(root).as_posix() for p in touched]
    if a.touched:
        Path(a.touched).write_text("\n".join(rel) + ("\n" if rel else ""), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"{'DRY RUN: ' if a.dry_run else ''}{len(rel)} file(s) changed, {sum(1 for n in notes if n.startswith('skipped'))} edit(s) already applied.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

## FILE: assets/aln-ui-redesign/README.txt (17 lines, 1436 bytes)

```
ALN UI Redesign components
========================
Made by the ALN UI Redesign Designer. All sizes are Minecraft GUI pixels (1 unit = 1 game pixel). Every PNG below is drawn at its true size.
Put the textures folder in the Resource Pack. design.json holds every value; preview_reference.png shows the intended result (4x zoom).

textures/ui/cheatstick/panel.png        8x8  nine-slice 3  (menu panel: fill, opacity 0.96, border 2, corners notched)
textures/ui/cheatstick/button.png       4x4  nine-slice 1  (normal button)
textures/ui/cheatstick/button_hover.png 4x4  nine-slice 1  (hover or highlighted button)
textures/ui/cheatstick/button_pressed.png 4x4  nine-slice 1  (pressed button)
textures/ui/cheatstick/title_bar.png    4x18  (flat bar, stretch across the panel width)
textures/ui/cheatstick/backdrop.png     1x1  (black at 0.5 opacity, stretch over the whole screen behind the menu)
Each .json next to a texture is its nine-slice description.

Layout (GUI pixels): panel width 190, padding 8, max height 210, title bar height 18, button height 20, gap between buttons 4, text under the title uses size 8.
Text: title name #ffaa00 + version #aaaaaa (center), body #b8bcc6, button text #ffffff (center), sizes 8/8/8. Use the Minecraft font; the preview font is only a stand-in.
Button icon slot: no. Raised bevel: yes (already drawn into the button textures).
Menus and their buttons are listed under 'screens' in design.json.
```

## FILE: assets/aln-ui-redesign/font/README.txt (13 lines, 792 bytes)

```
ALN UI Redesign - Pixelify Sans font (menu-only glyph page F0)

For any pack with a menu, form, HUD or screen that follows the ALN UI Redesign skin:
1. Copy glyph_F0.png into <pack>/resource_pack/font/ (copy, never link).
2. Copy pixelify.js into <pack>/behavior_pack/scripts/lib/.
3. Wrap menu strings: form.title(pixelify("...")), form.button(pixelify("...")).
   Spaces stay normal. Color codes (section sign + letter) stay outside pixelify().
4. Bump the Resource Pack version with manifest_tools.py --set.

Covers plain ASCII letters, digits, punctuation only. Vanilla font is untouched elsewhere.
Page F0 (U+F000-U+F0FF) is assumed unused by Minecraft; not tested in game.
Font: Pixelify Sans, SIL OFL 1.1 (see PixelifySans-OFL.txt). Keep that file with any pack that ships the glyphs.
```

## FILE: assets/aln-ui-redesign/font/pixelify.js (11 lines, 319 bytes)

```
// Pixelify Sans glyph mapper (page F0). Spaces and section-sign color codes stay vanilla.
const PAGE = 0x00F0;
export function pixelify(text) {
  let out = "";
  for (const ch of String(text)) {
    const c = ch.codePointAt(0);
    out += c > 0x20 && c < 0x7F ? String.fromCharCode(PAGE + c) : ch;
  }
  return out;
}
```
